## 46. When you let the AI write the ending to your favorite rom-com.

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/when-you-let-the-ai-write-the-ending-to-your-805567/video-2f248e906544.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/when-you-let-the-ai-write-the-ending-to-your-805567/poster-ceb0e82cd51f.jpg" alt="When you let the AI write the ending to your favorite rom-com. video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — [GENERATION GOAL] 15-second photorealistic cinematic short. Moody coastal drama on a storm-battered sea cliff. A young couple shares a wind-swept romantic moment as huge waves...</summary>

~~~~text
[GENERATION GOAL]
15-second photorealistic cinematic short. Moody coastal drama on a storm-battered sea cliff. A young couple shares a wind-swept romantic moment as huge waves explode against the rock. After the kiss and silhouette embrace, the man suddenly pushes the woman; she falls backward off the edge toward the churning sea. Prestige-drama cinematography: wide establish, intimate mediums and close-ups, silhouette, then an abrupt falling shot. No text, no logos, no extra people.

[GLOBAL STYLE]
Photorealistic live-action cinema. ARRI Alexa, 2x anamorphic, 2.39:1 letterboxed in 16:9. 24fps, fine film grain, slight halation on spray highlights, oval anamorphic bokeh. Color grade: desaturated steel-blue / slate-grey / wet charcoal, low saturation, crushed blacks, overcast volume light in the mist. Wet surfaces everywhere. Realistic wind on hair and fabric. No warmth, no sun shafts, no golden hour.

[CHARACTERS — lock for the full clip]
Man, late 20s, athletic lean-muscular build, short black hair faded on the sides, clean-shaven, warm tan skin, sharp features. Black pullover hoodie, dark charcoal cargo pants, dark sneakers.
Woman, mid-20s, long straight dark brown-black hair, olive-tan skin, striking features, dark eyes. Oversized slightly cropped white hoodie, light-wash denim shorts, white sneakers. Hair constantly lifted by wind and spray. She smiles and laughs first, then wide-eyed shock as she falls.

[ENVIRONMENT]
Remote high sea-cliff, Irish west-coast type. Dark layered wet basalt platform, sharp drop, no rail. Grey storm ocean, heavy swell, waves slamming the face and throwing tall white plumes higher than the couple. Low storm clouds, constant wind, mist and fine spray in the air. Wet rock reflecting the sky. No boats, no other people, no structures.

[TIMED SHOT BREAKDOWN]

0.0–2.2s — SHOT 1, extreme wide / wide, slightly elevated three-quarter from behind and to the side.
Couple small in frame on the wet rock edge, facing the ocean. A massive wave detonates against the cliff; a vertical wall of white spray rises and frames them. Wind pulls at their clothes. They turn toward each other, he takes her hand, both start to smile. Slow subtle dolly-in. Hard cut.

2.2–4.4s — SHOT 2, medium / medium-wide, light handheld energy.
They step together laughing. He lifts her into a spinning hug; her legs wrap loosely, white sneakers visible, long dark hair whipping, white hoodie lifting. Background is moving spray and grey water. Slow chest-height orbit. Hard cut.

4.4–6.6s — SHOT 3, medium close-up.
Tight embrace, both smiling, faces close. She wraps her arms around his neck. They kiss. Hair still moving. Shallow depth of field, spray soft behind them. Gentle handheld. Hard cut.

6.6–8.2s — SHOT 4, extreme close-up.
Kiss, then they ease back a few centimeters, foreheads almost touching, eyes opening. Soft rim from the mist. Hold the intimacy one beat. Hard cut.

8.2–10.0s — SHOT 5, medium-wide silhouette then the push.
Pull back. Dark silhouettes against the restless grey sea and storm sky. They stand close, her hand on his chest/neck. One beat of stillness. He suddenly shoves her toward the drop. She staggers back, arms flying out, face snapping from tenderness to shock. Waves keep crashing below. Hard cut on the shove.

10.0–14.5s — SHOT 6, falling shot, front-facing / slight high angle.
She is already falling backward off the edge. Arms flung wide, long dark hair flying upward in a wild halo, eyes wide on camera, mouth open in a shocked scream. White hoodie billowing. Background is rushing grey-green water, foam and spray filling the frame. Camera follows the fall with a slight downward tilt and push, face dominant first, then she recedes into mist and water. Hair, hoodie, and shorts react to wind and falling air.

14.5-15s — Fade to black.

[CAMERA]
Locked wide, slow dolly-in, gentle orbit, intimate handheld, then one falling follow. 35–50mm feel on wides and mediums, tighter on close-ups. Shallow-to-medium depth of field. Horizon mostly level until the fall. Hard cuts only between the listed shots — no whip pan except the cut into the fall.

[LIGHTING]
Overcast storm skylight only. Soft, directionless, slightly top-front. Volume from backlit spray and mist. Cool even light on faces. Wet rock speculars. No practicals, no warm sources.

[PHYSICS]
Constant strong wind: hair streams, hoodie ripples, shorts flutter. Spray hits clothes and skin. Wave plumes have real scale and weight. On the fall: hair and fabric behave like wet cloth in free air, not stiff. Skin stays wet. No morphing faces, no extra limbs.

[AUDIO]
Diegetic only. Deep ocean swell, wave crash, hiss of spray on rock, wind across the cliff, fabric snap. Closer wind and breath on the hug and kiss. On the push and fall: a short scuffle of shoes on wet stone, her sharp gasp, then rushing wind and the roar of water. No music, no score, no dialogue, no voiceover.

[CONSISTENCY LOCK]
Same two people, same clothes, same hair, same cliff and weather for the whole clip. White hoodie and denim shorts stay identical from first frame to last. Man stays in the black hoodie and dark cargos. No wardrobe change, no extra characters, no break in the clouds.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Photoreal travel cinema, 16:9 24fps, spherical 35–50mm, 180° shutter, fine grain, mild halation. Nordic teal-grey skies, moss greens, warm skin; gold only if sun is in frame. Soft contrast. Real wind/hair/weight. 9 shots, HARD CUTS only. No dissolves, drone, FPV, crane, orbit, beauty filter, plastic skin, HDR, logos, phones, signs. 21.0s realtime. Thin white centered sans captions. Hold last frame. Unhurried coastal Norway + alpine meadows. Sincere, not an ad.

LOCKS: bare face — no glasses/sunglasses on eyes. Vintage round goggles on forehead in B/C only, never on eyes. No hat ever (beanie, knit, red, silhouette). Hair down (A, 7–9) or rust-cream bandana (B/C).
HER=image1: mid-20s, slim-athletic 168–172cm, long wavy dark hair in wind, real teeth, quiet contentment → unforced grin. Same person all shots.
A (1,9): cream oversized knit, chocolate trousers, hair down, trail sneakers.
B (2,3): sage long-sleeve, rust-cream bandana, goggles on forehead, black pack, brown trousers, sneakers.
C (5,6): cream knit under teal-green vest, bandana + forehead goggles, brown trousers, sneakers.
C var (7,8): same vest, hair down.
Thin worn cord only; no logos.
HIM: one man, late 20s, lean, dark messy hair, lived-in stubble, muted green/grey layers. Shots 4,7,8 only. Silent.
Place: one N-European season — Lofoten granite into cold water, turf camps, misted hanging valleys, yellow-flower meadows, white/red fjord village. Milk/teal overcast except 7 sunset + 8 warm side-light.

1 0.0–2.2 CAMPSITE 35mm locked wide, slight rise. Grassy coastal camp, late overcast: green/rust/blue domes, dark-blue tunnel tent, jackets on grass. Atlantic left, granite massif. One seabird L→R upper third. HER A right third, three slow steps toward water, lifts face to the bird; hair/hem move. Tiny shore figures. Breath-handheld, no pan. END: still walking.

2 2.2–4.3 TRAIL 40mm. Dirt trail on grassy slope; teal fjord, sand crescent, tiny lot, red-roof farm below; milk overcast. HER B half-step to camera, right; pack tight, goggles on forehead, hair lifting. Turns to lens, real-eye smile. Slight follow. END: smiling mid-stride.

3 4.3–5.6 SHEEP 35mm 3/4 low. HER B crouched on berry-moss slope. One cream sheep, green ear tag. Palm on its back, one slow stroke, looking at it. Alpine lake in stone bowl behind, mist on ridge. END: stroke done.

4 5.6–8.2 MEADOW ground 24–28mm locked. Tall grass/flowers fill FG. HER+HIM on backs lower-left, heads close, toward sunlit peaks under green-gold sky. Light tee/layers; she shifts head, real breath. Spruce on walls. Grass may kiss lens. END: still lying.

5 8.2–11.2 FJORD 35mm follow from behind. HER C at dark still fjord; black-green peaks into water; harbor/vans far left. Two steps to water, arms wide palms-out, hair/vest lift. Pivots smiling, one arm toward lens. Eases to medium. Blur on turn only. END: faces camera, one arm out.

6 11.2–13.3 SUMMIT 35mm 3/4 from left. HER C sits onto pale granite (sit in-shot), knees in, tucks a strand, looks left along ridge then down-right at fjords, islands, white-red village. Private smile. Clouds drag peaks. Handheld eases closer. END: seated mid-look.

7 13.3–16.2 SUNSET low static 28mm. Two pack silhouettes L→R uphill through yellow globeflowers. Sun on ridge between them, orange flare into teal sky. HER right, hair loose, no hat outline, arms opening. HIM left, half-step back. FG flowers large/soft. END: mid-stride against sun.

8 16.2–19.0 BOOKS 40mm locked. Late meadow; pale mountain in warm side light beyond spruce. HER+HIM back-to-back in grass/yellow flowers, lower-right. She holds a paperback, head on his shoulder, turns a page, smiles at sky. He reads. END: page turned, still leaning.

9 19.0–21.0 BEACH static 50mm low. LOOK A. HER runs L→R on pale tidal beach, seaweed lines, hair streaming, wind in knit. Low mountains peach-sage dusk. Soft pink sea-thrift FG. Leg blur only. END: still running right, not exiting.

1/4/7/8/9 landscape+person; 2/5/6 human-forward, land still half. Breath-handheld only. Eyes sharp on faces; land sharp when land is the point.
Light: 1 cool coastal; 2 milk-white; 3 wet mist; 4 thin sun+grass bounce; 5 cool, faces readable; 6 open wrap; 7 sunset cutouts; 8 warm rake on stone; 9 peach dusk. No rim/fill/smoke.
Physics: wind in hair/knit/grass/straps; weighted steps; human gait; sheep has mass; pages turn by fingers; fjord water dark.

Audio: original sparse warm acoustic + low wordless hum. <surf/wind> <seabird> <boots grass then dirt> <sheep breath, hand on wool> <grass at lens> <wind as arms open> <two runners in flowers> <page, insects> <sneakers on wet sand>
VO close, unhurried, slight smile over 7–8, No speech, stock music, whooshes, known songs.

One woman image_1i, one man. Same face/hair/body/season/grade. No extra close characters, hats, glasses on her eyes, phones, drones, logos, CGI, plastic skin, warped hands, extra fingers, extra text, 9:16, statue poses in ⅙
~~~~

## 48. A cinematic creation film follows one maker reconstructing the main subject shown

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/a-cinematic-creation-film-follows-one-maker-reconstructing-the-main-311555/video-9615f0923d10.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/a-cinematic-creation-film-follows-one-maker-reconstructing-the-main-311555/poster-b10363323b51.jpg" alt="A cinematic creation film follows one maker reconstructing the main subject shown video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — A cinematic creation film follows one maker reconstructing the main subject shown in the referenced image completely from scratch. The referenced image defines the finished...</summary>

~~~~text
A cinematic creation film follows one maker reconstructing the main subject shown in the referenced image completely from scratch. The referenced image defines the finished subject’s complete visible appearance, proportions, structure, materials, colors, clothing or surface details, and distinctive features; ignore its background, framing, lighting, and unrelated elements.  Begin directly on an empty, clean virtual workbench. Use a stable front three-quarter overhead view that keeps the developing subject readable. There is one maker throughout, represented by the same consistent left and right hands and forearms. Show no more than two hands at once.  From 0 to 14 seconds, the entire creation unfolds as a clearly accelerated timelapse with rapid, purposeful hand movement and restrained motion blur. Short jump cuts compress repetitive manual work only after each action has visibly completed. Every cut inherits the exact form and progress left by the previous action.  0-2 seconds: One hand enters already holding the first foundation material, armature, or base element appropriate to the referenced subject and places it at the center. The second hand steadies it as the maker establishes the initial supporting form.  2-8 seconds: The maker rapidly develops the subject’s major structure and volumes using one coherent creation method appropriate to what the referenced image depicts. A living subject is sculpted as one continuous, non-gory digital form from armature to anatomy; a vehicle or machine is built from chassis to functional structure; an object is formed or assembled from its supporting body outward. Each additional material or component enters from outside the frame while firmly held by one of the maker’s hands, is carried to its destination, and remains under hand control until attached or shaped.  8-12 seconds: The same hands develop the recognizable outer form and reference-specific features. The maker sculpts, fits, wraps, stitches, fastens, carves, or polishes only where appropriate to the subject. Facial features, hair, clothing, body panels, wheels, glass, surfaces, accessories, or equivalent defining elements emerge through visible hand and tool contact, never through spontaneous transformation.  12-14 seconds: The maker refines proportions, edges, joints, surface transitions, textures, colors, and distinctive details until the developing subject closely matches the referenced image. One hand stabilizes the form while the other performs each final adjustment with a hand-held tool.  14-15 seconds: The maker removes the last tool by hand and withdraws both hands. The timelapse returns to normal speed as the camera makes a restrained push toward the completed subject and holds on a clean final view.  Materials and components do not need to be visible before use, but anything newly introduced must enter the frame already held by one of the maker’s hands. Nothing moves, assembles, appears, disappears, or changes material independently. Maintain one maker, one continuous subject, one creation position, and one category-appropriate construction method. Prompt idea ↓
~~~~

~~~~text
Create a 15-second, 16:9 widescreen, 24fps pure black-and-white Chinese ink-wash action short film. The overall visual style is a minimalist premium ink-wash cinematic title sequence: pure white background, extensive negative space, dense black character silhouettes, translucent gray-black ink smoke, and ultra-fine flowing ink filaments. All characters are NOT realistic humans. Every character is entirely formed from black ink, ink smoke, and delicate ink filaments, appearing as living humanoid figures condensed from liquid ink. HIGHEST PRIORITY — FACE DESIGN: Every character's face must remain extremely abstract, blurred, and completely devoid of recognizable facial features. NO VISIBLE FACIAL FEATURES. NO DETAILED EYES. NO VISIBLE PUPILS. NO DETAILED NOSE. NO DETAILED LIPS. NO FACIAL HIGHLIGHTS. NO FACIAL SHADOW MODELING. NO REALISTIC FACE. NO SKIN. NO SKIN COLOR. Never construct facial features through lighting or tonal contrast. No eye highlights, no illuminated nose bridge, no lips, no skin texture. Every face should look like a solid deep-black or dark-gray ink silhouette, preserving only an extremely vague outline of the head, jawline, or side profile. Facial features must appear completely swallowed by ink. Characters must be distinguished only through hats, hairstyles, conical hats, clothing silhouettes, body proportions, weapons, and movement — NEVER through facial features. The entire film contains only BLACK + WHITE + GRAY INK. Absolutely no skin tones and no color anywhere. 0–2.5s — WESTERN INK GUNSLINGER: A completely pure white empty space. Extremely thin and translucent black ink smoke drifts slowly into frame from the left. The smoke behaves like ink dispersing through clear water, creating delicate fluid strands that curl, twist, and intertwine. The strands converge toward the center and gradually form a tall Western gunslinger. Wide-brim cowboy hat, long trench coat, boots, hip holster, revolver. His entire body from head to toe consists exclusively of black and gray ink. Beneath the hat brim, the entire face is one deep-black ink shadow. Absolutely no eyes, nose, mouth, facial highlights, or skin. The lower edges of the trench coat are not fully solid; they dissolve into hundreds of translucent ink filaments trailing behind him. The gunslinger stands sideways with his head lowered, right hand hovering near the holster. Suddenly he performs an extremely stylish quick draw — draw the revolver, pivot sharply, raise the gun, then freeze completely for 0.15 seconds. BANG! He fires. The muzzle flash must be an extremely brief PURE WHITE impact flash only. No yellow or orange flame. The revolver produces strong physical recoil. At the exact instant of the shot — BONG! — the recoil shock violently travels backward through the gunslinger's body. His gun arm is the first to disintegrate into hundreds of long, thin ink filaments, followed sequentially by the shoulder, chest, trench coat, hat, torso, and legs. This is NOT an explosion of solid fragments. His body behaves like a concentrated mass of liquid ink suddenly blasted apart by an enormous gust of wind. Hundreds of black and gray ink-smoke strands accelerate violently along the direction of the shot and directly become the material used to form the next character. 2.5–5s — RONIN SWORDSMAN: The ink smoke from the previous gunslinger continues moving horizontally without a hard cut. The flowing strands curl, twist, contract, and intertwine in midair, gradually forming a completely different ronin swordsman. High-tied long hair, worn loose robe, waist sash, slender katana. The ends of his hair, shoulders, sleeves, and robe remain connected to translucent black ink filaments. His face is again a single deep-black ink mass. No eyes. No nose bridge highlight. No mouth. No facial modeling. The swordsman rapidly draws his blade. First strike: a fast diagonal upward slash from low to high. The blade leaves behind an extremely thin translucent black ink line. Second strike: his body rotates naturally into a rapid horizontal slash. His long hair and robe react with beautiful delayed secondary motion and physical inertia. He suddenly stops completely for 0.15 seconds. Both hands grip the sword. His body lowers into a powerful compressed stance. Clear anticipation and stored physical force. Suddenly explode forward. Final strike: an extremely violent diagonal heavy slash from lower left to upper right. WHOOSH! BONG! The strike appears powerful enough to cut through the entire pure-white canvas. A huge diagonal ink slash instantly tears across the frame. At the same moment, the swordsman's own body is pulled apart by the inertia of the strike. His arms, robe, torso, hair, and legs stretch into long black ink-smoke filaments and violently disintegrate along the sword's trajectory. The entire frame should feel as though the slash itself has physically sliced the composition apart. 5–7.5s — CONICAL-HAT WUXIA WARRIOR: The enormous diagonal ink trail from the previous slash keeps moving through the white space. No hard cut. The smoke bends into a smooth sweeping arc and gradually condenses into a third character: a mysterious masked wuxia warrior wearing an oversized conical hat, loose black martial-arts robes, extremely wide sleeves, long robe tails, waist belt, and holding a short staff. His face is completely hidden beneath the conical hat and a dense black ink shadow. Do NOT reveal his eyes. Do NOT create a sharp or intense gaze. His entire face must remain a featureless black ink silhouette. The warrior forms already in motion. He lands sideways with one foot striking the ground, immediately using the momentum to spin rapidly. His wide sleeves whip outward through centrifugal force. The sleeve edges dissolve into hundreds of transparent black ink strands, producing a huge circular ink-smoke trajectory around his body. He continues rotating, then suddenly swings the short staff violently across the camera. WHOOSH! The staff and wide sleeve pass extremely close to the lens. BONG! The resulting pressure wave instantly blows his entire body apart. The conical hat dissolves first, followed by the wide sleeves, shoulders, torso, waist, and legs, all stretched into high-speed black ink filaments. The character appears to be consumed by the violent wind generated by his own sweeping attack, completely dispersing toward the opposite side of the frame. 7.5–10s — FEMALE INK SPEAR WARRIOR: The ink smoke from the previous warrior does not disappear. Instead, the strands form an enormous rotating circular ring across the white frame. The ring gradually contracts toward its center and condenses into the fourth character: a female spear warrior. High ponytail, tall slender silhouette, fitted black wuxia clothing, trousers, lightweight flowing coat tails, and an extremely long black spear. The tip of her ponytail directly dissolves into ink smoke. Her face remains completely featureless. The entire facial plane is a flat deep-black ink silhouette. No eyes. No nose. No lips. No facial highlights. She spins the long spear rapidly with one hand, rotating it around her body for a complete circle. The spear tip creates an enormous circular black ink-smoke trajectory, with countless fine filaments flying elegantly through the pure white space. Suddenly the motion stops. She grips the spear with both hands, lowers her body, and holds a 0.15-second moment of visible anticipation. Then she violently pivots and unleashes a devastating horizontal spear sweep directly across the camera. WHOOSH! The spear shaft passes extremely close to the lens. BONG! The sweep generates an enormous wall-like wave of translucent black ink. At the same time, the female warrior is physically stretched apart along the spear's movement direction. Her ponytail, arms, waist, torso, clothing, and legs transform into long, delicate ink filaments. Her entire figure merges into the sweeping ink wave and exits the frame at high speed. 10–12.5s — HEAVY-BLADE CLOAKED WARRIOR: The giant black ink wave gradually becomes thinner and more transparent. Inside the fading wave, an enormous humanoid silhouette slowly emerges. The fifth character condenses from the ink: a towering heavy-blade warrior, visibly taller and broader than every previous character. He wears an enormous damaged black cloak and carries a massive broad-bladed sword. His face is a completely solid deep-black ink shadow with absolutely no facial features. Never use lighting to reveal his face. The cloak is extremely important: near the shoulders it is dense, solid black ink, but as it extends backward it gradually becomes translucent until the edges separate into hundreds of long, fine ink-smoke strands. The warrior initially stands with his back toward the camera, dragging the heavy blade. He slowly turns sideways. Suddenly he pivots violently. His enormous cloak whips upward with strong physical inertia. Using his entire body weight, he performs one powerful 360-degree spinning heavy slash. WHOOSH! The broad blade sweeps across nearly the entire frame and generates a gigantic semicircular ink arc. BONG! At the exact moment the blade crosses the frame, the cloak is ripped into smoke by the rotational inertia. Then the shoulders, torso, arms, waist, and legs violently disintegrate. The entire massive warrior is thrown outward into one enormous rotating black smoke ribbon, which continues spinning through the pure white space and naturally becomes the transition material for the final character. 12.5–15s — NAMELESS INK WARRIOR, FINAL SLASH: All rotating black ink smoke suddenly decelerates. Hundreds of fine ink filaments float suspended inside the pure white space. Complete stillness for 0.15 seconds. Then, instantly, every filament reverses direction. Hundreds of black ink strands rush violently toward the center of the frame and condense into the final nameless warrior. His silhouette must NOT repeat any previous character. No hat. No conical hat. No cloak. Slightly long, messy black ink hair. Minimalist long black robe. Tall slender physique. One hand holding a minimalist long blade. His entire face remains hidden inside a featureless black ink shadow. NO FACIAL FEATURES. The warrior stands silently with his head lowered. The camera slowly pushes forward. His hair tips and clothing edges gently release extremely thin drifting ink strands. Suddenly he raises his head, but absolutely no eyes, nose, mouth, or facial structure become visible. Only a featureless black human-shaped face silhouette remains. Hold for 0.15 seconds. In the next instant, he violently steps directly toward the camera while simultaneously drawing his sword. An extremely fast horizontal slash. WHOOSH! The blade cuts directly toward the camera. BONG!!! This is the strongest impact of the entire film. The blade sweeps horizontally across the entire 16:9 frame. The warrior's body instantly disintegrates following the direction of the slash. Hair, head, shoulders, arms, robe, torso, and legs transform into thousands of ultra-fine black ink-smoke strands. Massive quantities of ink filaments rush directly toward the camera and briefly cover the frame. Then they rapidly disperse. The black ink becomes progressively lighter. The smoke becomes increasingly transparent. Eventually only a few extremely faint gray-black filaments remain floating against the white background. They slowly disappear completely. Final frame: PURE WHITE. MATERIAL LOCK: Every character must always consist of solid black ink core + translucent gray-black smoke edges + ultra-fine ink filaments. The central body mass is dense black ink. Toward the edges, the body gradually becomes transparent. The outermost silhouette dissolves into numerous extremely fine, lightweight black smoke filaments. Ink smoke must behave like liquid ink dispersing through clear water: lightweight, translucent, filament-like, elegant, fluid, organic, and continuously flowing. Never use thick black paint. Never cover the entire frame with random heavy ink splashes. Maintain extensive pure white negative space throughout the entire film. CHARACTER DIFFERENTIATION: Gunslinger = wide-brim hat + long trench coat + revolver. Ronin swordsman = high-tied long hair + worn robe + katana. Wuxia warrior = oversized conical hat + wide sleeves + short staff. Female warrior = high ponytail + fitted wuxia clothing + long spear. Cloaked warrior = massive physique + enormous damaged cloak + heavy broad blade. Nameless warrior = messy medium-long hair + minimalist long robe + long blade. All six characters must be instantly distinguishable through silhouette, clothing, weapon, hairstyle, body proportions, and movement. NEVER distinguish them through facial features. ACTION RHYTHM: The entire film follows the rhythm of effortless elegance → brief stillness → sudden explosive acceleration → BONG impact → ink-smoke disintegration → next character condensation. Movements must NOT remain constantly fast. Every major attack must contain a very brief moment of absolute stillness beforehand. Use dramatic speed ramps only during attack bursts. Every weapon must feel physically heavy and possess believable momentum. Clothing, long hair, cloak fabric, weapons, and ink smoke must have realistic inertia, drag, delayed secondary motion, and follow-through. The direction in which one character disintegrates must naturally continue into the direction from which the next character forms. The entire 15-second sequence must feel like one continuous choreographed ink-wash action performance rather than six separate clips. TRANSITIONS MUST BE PHYSICAL AND MOTION-DRIVEN: character disintegration → traveling ink filaments → reorganization → next character formation. No automatic morphing. No generic dissolve. No fade transition. No hard cut. ABSOLUTE NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS: NO VISIBLE FACIAL FEATURES. NO DETAILED FACE. NO FACE LIGHTING. NO FACIAL HIGHLIGHTS. NO FACIAL SHADOW MODELING. NO EYE HIGHLIGHTS. NO VISIBLE PUPILS. NO DETAILED EYES. NO DETAILED NOSE. NO DETAILED LIPS. NO SKIN. NO SKIN COLOR. NO COLORED FACE. NO BEIGE. NO PINK. NO BROWN. NO COLOR ANYWHERE. NO REALISTIC HUMAN FACE. NO PHOTOREALISTIC SKIN. NO TEXT. NO SUBTITLES. NO CAPTIONS. NO LOGO. NO WATERMARK. NO UI. NO ORANGE MUZZLE FLASH. NO YELLOW MUZZLE FLASH. NO FIRE. NO FIRE EXPLOSION. NO BUILDINGS. NO FOREST. NO ROOM. NO LANDSCAPE. NO REALISTIC ENVIRONMENT. NO ANIME. NO CARTOON. NO CYBERPUNK. NO MAGIC SPELLS. NO ENERGY BEAMS. NO GLOWING MAGIC EFFECTS. NO RANDOM CHARACTER MORPHING. NO AUTOMATIC DISSOLVE. NO SIMPLE FADE. NO HARD CUT. MOST IMPORTANT RULE: Under absolutely no circumstances should the model reconstruct, complete, illuminate, or reveal any character's facial features. Every face must remain a single deep-black, blurred, flat ink silhouette without facial highlights, facial shadows, eyes, pupils, nose definition, lips, skin, or skin color from the first frame to the final frame. All charisma, identity, visual appeal, and character differentiation must come entirely from body silhouette, clothing, hairstyle, weapons, posture, and movement. From the first frame to the final frame, the only visual elements allowed are a PURE WHITE BACKGROUND and BLACK/GRAY INK.
~~~~

~~~~text
[Background / Floor] Create a gigantic background surface spanning the entire area immediately behind the performers, extending horizontally across the frame. integrated_multimodal_description:
~~~~

~~~~text
<Image 1> is the CHARACTER APPEARANCE REFERENCE.
<Image 0> is the BACKGROUND LOCATION REFERENCE.

Use <Image 1> to preserve the man's exact facial identity, age, dark hair with gray temples, rugged stubble, body proportions, green-and-black tactical suit, illuminated chest emblem, and power-ring design.

Use <Image 0> to preserve the open landscape, terrain, lighting, atmosphere, and general environmental appearance.

CRITICAL RIGHT-HAND CONTINUITY:
The power ring is worn ONLY on the man's anatomical RIGHT HAND throughout the entire video.
NEVER place the ring on his left hand.
Do NOT mirror or swap hands when the camera angle changes.
His LEFT HAND remains completely ringless throughout the entire sequence.
Every green energy projection originates directly from the ring on his RIGHT HAND.

Photorealistic live-action cinematic realism.

Environment: vast isolated American desert-like prairie, dry golden grass, dusty earth, scattered low scrub, distant low hills, huge open sky. Warm late-afternoon sunlight. No buildings, existing vehicles, roads, or other people visible.

The ONLY vehicle appearing in the scene is the futuristic sports-car construct created by the man's ring.

[00:00–00:02.0]

Wide cinematic tracking shot.

The man from <Image 1> walks casually through the open landscape.

His arms swing naturally as he walks.

After several steps he slows and stops.

He looks across the empty landscape in front of him as though deciding how he wants to travel.

Camera gently moves from the wider walking shot into a medium three-quarter view.

[00:02.0–00:03.0]

Medium shot.

He turns slightly toward an open patch of ground beside him.

He raises his anatomical RIGHT HAND and deliberately points the power ring toward the empty space.

His LEFT hand remains completely ringless.

The ring begins glowing with concentrated emerald-green energy.

[00:03.0–00:06.5]

A narrow, clearly defined GREEN ENERGY BEAM shoots directly from the ring on his RIGHT HAND into the empty space beside him.

CRITICAL:
From this moment through the FINAL FRAME, the green energy beam remains visibly and continuously connected between his RIGHT-HAND ring and the construct sports car.

The beam NEVER disconnects.
The beam NEVER disappears.
The beam NEVER switches hands.
The beam NEVER originates from his left hand.

Camera shifts to a wider three-quarter angle so both the man and the forming vehicle remain clearly visible.

The beam begins DRAWING A FUTURISTIC SPORTS CAR INTO EXISTENCE like a three-dimensional emerald sketch.

The construction is clearly progressive and physically understandable.

Bright emerald lines rapidly trace:

wheels → lower chassis → aerodynamic nose → side profile → rear body → windshield and cockpit → roofline → remaining body panels.

The glowing wireframe establishes the complete shape of a low, sleek, aggressive futuristic sports car.

As each section is drawn, the wireframe rapidly fills and solidifies into dense, physical-looking emerald-green construct energy.

The vehicle is NOT an ordinary green-painted automobile.

It is unmistakably a POWER-RING ENERGY CONSTRUCT: solid and physically usable, but composed entirely of luminous translucent emerald-green energy with subtle internal glowing structure.

Four clearly defined wheels.
Believable tires made from solid green construct energy.
Low aerodynamic body.
Futuristic cockpit.
Sleek windshield.
Aggressive but elegant proportions.

The DRIVER-SIDE DOOR AREA deliberately remains unfinished and open while the rest of the vehicle completes.

[00:06.5–00:08.0]

The sports car finishes forming except for the open driver's entrance.

The man lowers his right arm slightly while STILL maintaining the visible energy connection between his RIGHT-HAND ring and the construct car.

He looks over the completed futuristic car.

A confident, slightly cocky smirk appears on his face.

Hold just long enough to clearly read his expression.

No dialogue.

[00:08.0–00:10.5]

Camera moves into a dynamic medium-wide side angle.

The man walks directly to the open driver's side.

The continuous green energy beam remains connected from his RIGHT-HAND ring to the car throughout his movement.

He naturally steps into the driver's seat.

Do NOT make him teleport into the vehicle.

Do NOT have him pass through the bodywork.

He physically enters through the intentionally unfinished driver's-side opening and sits behind the wheel.

Once he is fully seated, glowing construct lines rapidly draw and solidify the final driver's-side door around him, completing the sports car.

The green beam STILL remains connected from the ring on his anatomical RIGHT HAND to the car.

[00:10.5–00:12.0]

Low cinematic three-quarter shot of the completed vehicle.

The futuristic emerald construct sports car powers up.

Internal green energy pulses subtly through its body.

The wheels begin turning.

Dust shifts beneath the tires.

Through the cockpit, the man gives another brief confident, cocky smirk.

His RIGHT HAND remains the hand sustaining the construct.

The continuous green energy connection remains active.

[00:12.0–00:15.0]

The construct sports car launches forward with powerful acceleration.

Real dust and loose dirt kick backward from beneath the glowing green construct wheels.

Camera briefly tracks alongside at a low angle as the vehicle rapidly gains speed.

A beautiful EMERALD-GREEN ENERGY TRAIL streams behind the construct car as it accelerates across the open landscape.

The car sweeps past the camera and drives COMPLETELY OUT OF FRAME.

Do NOT stop the car before it exits.

Do NOT dissolve the vehicle.

Do NOT transform it into another object.

The construct remains a complete futuristic sports car as it leaves the shot.

CRITICAL FINAL CONTINUITY:
The sports car remains actively sustained by the man's RIGHT-HAND power ring while he drives.

The visible green energy connection remains continuously linked to his RIGHT-hand ring and integrated into the construct car's glowing energy structure through the FINAL FRAME.

The ring NEVER appears on his left hand.

No dialogue.
No attack.
No explosion.
No additional constructs.
No additional characters.
No existing real-world vehicles.

overall_soundscape:

Natural open-land ambience with gentle dry wind moving through grass and subtle footsteps on dusty earth at the beginning.

When the RIGHT-HAND ring activates, introduce a clean concentrated emerald-energy hum.

The three-dimensional drawing process produces precise crystalline energy sounds as glowing lines trace the vehicle into existence, followed by deeper resonant energy tones as the wireframe solidifies into the physical construct.

Once completed, the sports car produces a smooth futuristic energy-powered motor sound rather than a conventional gasoline engine.

Subtle rising energy whine as the vehicle powers up.

Construct tires interact physically with the terrain: dirt crunch, tire movement, and a strong burst of dust and gravel as the car accelerates.

The energy motor rises rapidly in pitch as the car drives away, accompanied by a subtle sustained energy sound from the emerald trail.

Natural wind and landscape ambience remain underneath.

No dialogue.

non_diegetic_music:

None.
~~~~

~~~~text
integrated_multimodal_description:

[Visual Anchor]
A photorealistic cinematic 8-second sequence inside a modern luxury high-speed train. The woman remains seated comfortably beside the panoramic windows while the train continuously moves forward at normal high-speed rail velocity.

ONLY THE WORLD OUTSIDE THE TRAIN travels backward through history.

The train interior NEVER changes.

Her clothing NEVER changes.

She breathes naturally, occasionally blinks, subtly follows the landscape with her eyes, and gives a faint knowing smile. She is never frightened, startled, confused, or alarmed. She behaves as though she already knew this would happen.

The historical effect uses a repeating rhythm:

STABLE ERA → RAPID BACKWARD TIME-LAPSE TRANSFORMATION → STABLE ERA → RAPID BACKWARD TIME-LAPSE TRANSFORMATION.

During each stable era, the exterior world moves and behaves at NORMAL REAL-TIME SPEED while the train continues traveling through it.

During each transition, ONLY the exterior environment rapidly rewinds through time.

[00:00.000–00:01.000 — PRESENT DAY]

Begin EXACTLY from <Picture 1>.

The modern train moves naturally forward through the cold snowy alpine landscape.

Strong realistic parallax is visible outside: nearby snow-covered terrain moves quickly past the windows while the distant mountains move more slowly.

The woman quietly watches the scenery.

Natural breathing.
One subtle blink.
Small relaxed eye movement.

The snowy mountain landscape remains stable long enough for the viewer to recognize the present-day setting.

[00:01.000–00:01.500 — FIRST TIME REWIND]

The ENTIRE exterior world suddenly begins rapidly traveling backward through time like an accelerated environmental time-lapse.

There is NO camera cut.

Snow rapidly retreats across the entire landscape.

Frozen ground becomes exposed earth.

Grass spreads rapidly.

Trees and vegetation appear and grow across the mountains and valleys.

The cold white alpine environment smoothly transforms into a lush green temperate landscape.

The transformation happens across ALL visible windows simultaneously.

The woman and modern train remain completely unchanged and continue moving in normal real-time.

[00:01.500–00:02.500 — LUSH GREEN LAND]

The time-lapse transformation STOPS.

The exterior is now a rich green countryside.

Forests, grassy hills, streams, wild vegetation, and distant mountains move naturally past the train.

Everything outside now behaves at normal real-time speed.

Leaves move in the wind.
Water flows naturally.
The train continues traveling forward.

The woman calmly watches and gives a very slight peaceful smile.

[00:02.500–00:03.000 — SECOND TIME REWIND]

The exterior world rapidly rewinds again.

The green countryside transforms through accelerated historical change.

Roads appear as dirt tracks.

Old fences develop.

Buildings rapidly emerge into 19th-century construction.

The entire landscape transitions smoothly into the 1800s.

No cut.

[00:03.000–00:04.100 — 1800s]

The transformation STOPS.

The train now passes through a living 19th-century town and countryside.

The exterior operates at NORMAL SPEED.

Horse-drawn carriages travel along dirt streets.

Horses walk and trot naturally.

People wearing 1800s clothing move along sidewalks and streets.

Old masonry and timber buildings, storefronts, chimneys, wooden fences, wagons, and period street activity pass naturally outside.

NO modern vehicles.
NO modern signs.
NO modern technology outside.

The train continues moving rapidly through the historical environment.

The woman remains relaxed and simply watches.

[00:04.100–00:04.600 — THIRD TIME REWIND]

History rapidly rewinds again.

The 1800s town begins disappearing through a smooth accelerated time-lapse.

Buildings regress and vanish.

Roads deteriorate into ancient tracks.

Modern-style structures disappear completely.

The landscape rapidly transforms into the ancient Roman era.

The transformation occurs across the ENTIRE exterior view simultaneously.

[00:04.600–00:05.800 — ROMAN ERA]

The transformation STOPS.

The train now passes beside a large ancient battlefield.

Everything outside moves at NORMAL REAL-TIME SPEED.

Roman soldiers in historically inspired armor fight opposing barbarian warriors across an open field.

Shields collide.
Spears move.
Banners flap in the wind.

The battle remains at a believable distance from the train and remains entirely outside.

No soldier notices the train.
No soldier approaches the windows.
No one interacts with the woman.

The woman watches the battle calmly.

[00:05.800–00:06.300 — MASSIVE TIME REWIND]

The exterior suddenly accelerates backward across an enormous span of history.

The Roman soldiers rapidly disappear.

Battlefield activity vanishes.

Roads disappear.

Structures disappear.

Human civilization disappears completely.

Vegetation, terrain, climate, and geological features rapidly transform in a massive backward time-lapse spanning millions of years.

[00:06.300–00:08.000 — PREHISTORIC WORLD]

The transformation STOPS.

The train is now traveling through a vast untouched prehistoric landscape.

Everything outside returns to NORMAL REAL-TIME SPEED.

No humans.

No buildings.
No roads.

Several dinosaurs move naturally in the DISTANT landscape.

A huge long-necked dinosaur slowly walks across the far background.

Smaller dinosaurs move farther away among ancient vegetation.

Keep the dinosaurs at believable distances.

NO dinosaur approaches the train.
NO dinosaur looks through the windows.
NO dinosaur attacks.

The train continues traveling forward normally through this prehistoric world.

The woman quietly watches the dinosaurs.

Near the final moment, she gives a subtle knowing smile.

End while the train is still moving through the prehistoric landscape.

[CRITICAL WINDOW CONTINUITY]

ALL panoramic train windows show ONE SINGLE CONTINUOUS EXTERIOR WORLD.

The windows must NOT behave like separate television screens.

Do NOT place different eras in different window panes.

overall_soundscape:
~~~~

~~~~text
Create a 15-second viral-ready stylized 3D animation with a bold graphic-poster aesthetic, combining 3D cel shading, abstract motion graphics, surreal transformations, and extremely dynamic camera movement.

STYLE: Stylized 3D toon/cel-shaded animation, low-poly geometric forms, hard-edged shadows, clean surfaces, exaggerated proportions, strong silhouettes, minimal textures, high contrast. Make the world feel like a graphic poster transformed into 3D space. Avoid photorealism and generic CGI.

COLOR PALETTE: Golden yellow, amber, lemon yellow, black, and white dominate the entire video. Metallic gray appears only as a small accent. Red is allowed only on the villain's sofa, liquor, and warning signals.

0–3 SECONDS — INSTANT HOOK:
Open with an extreme close-up of a mysterious 3D graphic object rushing directly toward the camera. Suddenly pull the camera backward to reveal a surreal geometric city made of pipes, grids, sliced buildings, blocks, signs, and mechanical structures. Use a dramatic perspective shift and rapid camera rotation.

3–7 SECONDS — ESCALATION:
The city begins transforming at impossible speed. Buildings split into geometric pieces, pipes twist through the streets, blocks rotate in mid-air, and graphic symbols explode outward. The camera flies through the environment with aggressive parallax, speed ramps, and seamless transitions.

7–11 SECONDS — CHARACTER + SURPRISE:
Reveal the villain sitting on a distinctive red sofa in the middle of the moving city. The villain makes one dramatic gesture. Instantly, the sofa, surrounding architecture, and entire environment transform into a massive surreal 3D geometric structure. Use a rapid push-in followed by a sudden wide shot.

11–14 SECONDS — CLIMAX:
Everything accelerates. Yellow and black geometric elements spiral around the camera. Buildings fold, rotate, compress, and reconstruct. Use extreme perspective, fast tracking, graphic match cuts, silhouettes, diagonal masks, distortion, and a powerful final camera movement.

14–15 SECONDS — LOOP:
The entire world rapidly collapses into the same graphic object from the opening shot. End on the exact visual composition needed to seamlessly connect back to the first frame, creating a perfect infinite loop.

ANIMATION LANGUAGE: Constant motion, squash and stretch, geometric morphing, slicing, flipping, compression, distortion, object transformation, impossible physics, speed ramps, freeze-frame accents, and satisfying visual cause-and-effect.

CAMERA: Extreme push-ins, rapid pull-outs, rotating shots, overhead views, low angles, impossible tracking shots, camera-through-object transitions, sudden perspective changes, and dramatic scale shifts.

FINAL LOOK: Premium, surreal, energetic, highly stylized 3D cel animation that feels like a living graphic poster. Every second must introduce a new visual surprise. Make the first second immediately attention-grabbing and the final second perfectly loopable.
~~~~

</details>

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a 15-second ultra-photorealistic live-action war sequence set in the United States in 1947, designed to look like authentic historical footage captured on a 1940s film...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a 15-second ultra-photorealistic live-action war sequence set in the United States in 1947, designed to look like authentic historical footage captured on a 1940s film camera. The entire scene must feel grounded, documentary-like, raw, and physically realistic.
Environment: A rural American town in 1947 with wooden houses, old brick buildings, telephone poles, dirt roads, vintage American cars from the 1940s, wooden fences, farmland, and period-accurate street details. Overcast afternoon light, light fog, drifting smoke, dust in the air, damaged buildings, scattered debris, and a tense wartime atmosphere.
Characters: American soldiers wearing historically accurate late-1940s military uniforms, helmets, boots, and equipment. Civilians wear authentic 1940s American clothing. Natural faces, realistic skin texture, sweat, dirt, fatigue, and believable body movements.
0–3s — Establishing Shot:
Wide handheld shot of a quiet rural American street suddenly filled with smoke and confusion. Vintage 1940s vehicles are parked along the road while soldiers move quickly between wooden buildings. Civilians rush toward safer areas.
3–6s — Tension:
Camera moves through the street at shoulder height, following several soldiers as distant gunfire is heard. They immediately react and take cover behind a vintage vehicle and a brick wall. Their movements are cautious and realistic.
6–10s — Combat:
Fast handheld tracking shot as the soldiers move between cover while distant gunfire impacts the environment. Small pieces of wood, dust, and debris fall naturally from nearby impacts. Weapon recoil, movement, and body weight must be physically accurate. Keep the violence realistic and restrained.
10–13s — Human Moment:
Camera briefly focuses on a soldier helping an injured civilian move behind cover. Their breathing, facial expressions, body language, and movement should feel natural and unscripted.
13–15s — Final Shot:
Camera pulls back into a wide shot of the American town as smoke slowly moves through the street. Soldiers remain behind cover while vintage vehicles and damaged buildings fill the background. The scene ends with an authentic, tense 1940s documentary feeling.
Visual Style: Ultra-photorealistic live-action, authentic 1940s American environment, vintage 35mm film texture, subtle film grain, natural imperfections, realistic exposure, handheld documentary cinematography, muted historical color palette, realistic smoke and dust, natural shadows, accurate depth of field.
Physics: Strictly obey real-world gravity, momentum, inertia, friction, recoil, weight, collision physics, and human biomechanics. No exaggerated explosions, impossible movements, superhero behavior, or choreographed-looking combat.
Negative Prompt: modern buildings, modern cars, smartphones, modern clothing, modern weapons, futuristic technology, CGI appearance, video-game graphics, fantasy, superhero action, excessive explosions, excessive blood, gore, impossible physics, unrealistic recoil, slow-motion physics, distorted faces, extra limbs, floating objects, plastic skin, artificial-looking environments.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Haru: teen boy, short messy dark-brown hair with warm amber rim-light on the
edges, tired amber eyes, single silver earring on left ear, thin dark cord

Anime television opening, 2D cel-shaded, crisp linework, 16:9, high contrast,
film grain. Total runtime 15 seconds, 12 shots, beat-synced hard cuts.

[SHOT LIST — 15s]
00.0–01.2  Black. Hard white flash. Haru's silhouette snaps in, back to camera,
           jacket billowing. Background floods CYAN.
01.2–02.4  Whip-pan to profile close-up. Downcast eyes flick up, sharp.

07.4–08.2  Hands-in-pockets front shot, camera orbits fast around him.

08.2–09.4  Seated on the ledge, knees up, head down, city lights blooming

09.4–10.6  He stands, turns to camera, hair lifting, single amber rim-light
           igniting along his edge. Background goes pure WHITE.
10.6–15.0  Pull-back wide: Haru walks away toward the sunset skyline, hands in
           pockets, clothes drifting. Color settles into the warm painted
           palette of the reference sheet. Camera dollies out, holds on
           centered title-card space.

[MOTION]
Simple punchy character animation on twos. Hair and fabric always drifting.
Camera: whip pans, snap zooms, one fast orbit, one final dolly-out.

[COLOR & FX]
Full-frame color swaps on every cut, synced to an implied beat. White
flash-frames between shots 1–9. Chromatic aberration on impacts, halftone dot
bursts, quick duotone inversions, floating leaf and paper debris throughout.

changing outfit, changing hair color, distorted face, extra fingers, watermark
~~~~

## 65. An 1890s field guide plate, drawing itself

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Hailuo Minimax H3 prompt @ Image1 is the sole visual authority for the folio spread, the wren, the hazel twig, the egg study, the foot sketch, the handwriting, the printed type,...</summary>

~~~~text
Hailuo Minimax H3 prompt

@ Image1 is the sole visual authority for the folio spread, the wren, the hazel twig, the egg study, the foot sketch, the handwriting, the printed type, the paper, the foxing, the lighting, the palette, the layout and the horizontal composition.

Animate this exact page without redesigning it. Preserve every existing mark, letter and colour in its final state.

[Goal] Create a 15-second sequence in which this plate makes itself, and then the bird inside it comes quietly alive. One continuous, locked, straight-on overhead composition. No camera movement, no cuts, no cropping, no perspective change, no page turning.

[Sequence]  0-2.4 seconds: Begin on blank aged ivory paper — the empty spread, correct in paper texture, chain lines, foxing and lighting, but carrying no image, no writing and no type. A faint graphite underdrawing appears across the right page in light searching strokes, finding the wren's posture and the line of the twig.
A few construction marks overshoot and are left uncorrected.

2.4-6 seconds: Engraved ink linework draws itself over the pencil, following the exact contours of the finished plate — outline first, then hatching building the shadow under the wing, the barring of the tail, the texture of the twig. The rectangular plate mark presses into the paper around the image with a soft physical impression, lifting the paper very slightly at its edge.

6-10.5 seconds: Watercolour floods in. Washes enter the printed outlines and settle into them, warm russet across the back, buff along the eyebrow stripe, muted olive on the twig, each wash landing exactly within the shapes of the finished plate and overrunning the line in the same few places the reference does.

The colour blooms wet and then visibly dries, darkening slightly at the edges. On the left page, the sepia handwriting writes itself line by line from the top, in a period cursive hand that is elegant, cramped and only partly legible. The small egg study fills with colour and the pencil foot sketch appears faintly beside it.
10.5-13 seconds: The printed type impresses into the right page — TROGLODYTES TROGLODYTES, then The Common Wren beneath it, then PLATE XVII in the top right corner. Foxing spots deepen. The last details resolve: the highlight in the bird's eye, the fine barring on the flank. The page settles. Everything on the spread is now exactly as in @[ref img].  13-15 seconds: The page stays completely still and completely flat

— it is paper and it behaves like paper. But the printed bird begins to breathe. Its flank rises and falls very slightly. It blinks once. It turns its head a few degrees and holds, alert, looking past the viewer at something outside the page. The cocked tail gives one small twitch. It does not fly, it does not leave the plate, it does not become three-dimensional, and it does not look at the viewer. Settle into a living idle: the bird breathing, one more slow blink, the paper still.  Render as photoreal archival photography of a real object under warm north-window light. Keep the printed page flat and matte with no glow, no emission, no glint and no digital sheen.

Keep the two motion layers distinct: the page is a made thing being made, the bird is alive. Keep all type, handwriting, marks and geometry fixed once they appear.  Do not introduce any new text, letters, numerals, marks, birds, objects, hands, tools, logos, captions, borders, watermarks or page numbers. Do not turn or lift the page. Do not move, rotate or scale the camera. Do not zoom. Do not add glow, neon, coloured light, lens flare, bokeh or shallow-focus racking.

Do not make the bird photoreal or three-dimensional — it must remain a hand-coloured engraving that happens to be alive. Do not have it fly, hop, open its beak wide, or leave the plate.

Audio: fine graphite scratching on rough paper; the dry precise scrape of a steel nib; the deep soft compression of a plate press; a sable brush loaded with water moving across paper; paper fibres settling; the quiet room tone of a study with a slow long-case clock ticking somewhere behind.
At 13 seconds, one Eurasian wren song — sudden, intricate and far louder than anything that small should produce — then the room tone returns and the clock keeps going. No music, no dialogue, no narration, no electronic tones.
~~~~

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — @ Image1 is the sole authority for the PROOF logo: the roundel of five concentric rings and the PROOF wordmark in heavy geometric rounded sans. Reproduce the mark and the spelling...</summary>

~~~~text
@ Image1 is the sole authority for the PROOF logo: the roundel of five concentric rings
and the PROOF wordmark in heavy geometric rounded sans. Reproduce the mark and the
spelling P-R-O-O-F exactly and never alter, restyle or re-letter them. @ Image2 is the
authority for the branded kraft bakery bag. @ Image3 is the authority for the courier,
the canvas bread satchel and the dawn street.

[GOAL]
A 15-second vertical 9:16 social launch commercial for PROOF, a dawn bakery delivery
service. Fast-cut, caption-led, designed to be understood with the sound off. Photoreal
documentary commercial cinematography on 35mm, fine grain, shallow depth of field,
handheld with restrained movement.

[THE LIGHT IS THE CLOCK]
The film brightens continuously from beginning to end. It opens in near-darkness lit by
a single work lamp and finishes in full warm golden sunrise. Every shot is measurably
lighter than the one before it. This progression must be unbroken.

[CAPTION CHIPS]
Throughout, a single caption chip sits in the lower third, horizontally centred, in the
same position in every shot. It is a rounded rectangle in burnt ochre with warm cream
text, two lines: a lighter-weight top line and a bold bottom line. The chip changes
content on the cuts listed below and never moves, resizes or animates. Type must be
crisp and correctly spelled.

[SEQUENCE — cut on every timing, eight cuts]
0-1.9s    HERO. Interior of a small bakery at night, lit by one overhead work lamp.
          A baker's hands turn a large mass of wet dough on a floured steel bench.
          Everything else in the room is black.
          CHIP: "04:12" / "someone is already awake"
1.9-3.6s  INSERT. Extreme close-up, a handful of flour thrown across the bench, dust
          catching the single lamp.
          CHIP unchanged.
3.6-5.4s  HERO. A row of round dough balls resting in floured proving baskets on a rack.
          Slightly warmer, slightly brighter. One dough is visibly swollen and domed.
          CHIP: "05:30" / "it's proving"
5.4-7.1s  INSERT. An oven door swings open and a wall of steam rolls out toward camera,
          lit warm from inside.
          CHIP unchanged.
7.1-8.9s  HERO. A dark-crusted round sourdough loaf lands on a cooling rack. Hold on it.
          The crust is visibly crackling and ticking as it cools.
          CHIP: "06:15" / "it's out of the oven"
8.9-10.6s INSERT. The loaf is slid into the kraft PROOF bag from @ Image2, the top folded
          over once and taped. The ochre roundel and wordmark clearly readable.
          CHIP unchanged.
10.6-12.3s HERO. The courier from @ Image3 rides away down an empty terraced street in
          low golden sunrise, satchel on their back, PROOF mark readable, streetlights
          switching off behind them.
          CHIP: "06:52" / "it's moving"
12.3-13.6s INSERT. The kraft bag set down on a stone doorstep in full golden light,
          exactly as in @ Image2. A hand withdraws from frame. Steam rises from the fold.
          CHIP: "07:04" / "it's at your door"
13.6-15s  END FRAME. Cut to a clean flat warm cream background. The PROOF concentric
          ring mark and wordmark from @ Image1 sit centred, still, in burnt ochre and
          dark rye brown. Beneath them, small, in dark rye brown: "now delivering".
          CHIP for the final beat only: "bread younger than your alarm", single bold
          line, centred. Absolutely no motion on this frame — it is a still card.

[COLOUR]
Burnt ochre is a physical object in the world before it is ever an interface colour —
it is printed ink on the kraft bag and on the canvas satchel, lit by real light. The
caption chip is the only ochre graphic element. Palette: warm cream, kraft brown, burnt
ochre, dark rye brown, one cool blue-grey in the pre-dawn shadows.

[AUDIO]
Diegetic sound only, no music of any kind. The mix should feel close, warm and real.
0-3.6s    The dead quiet of a room at 4am: an extractor fan humming, wet dough slapping
          and stretching against steel, a scraper, the soft thud of flour.
3.6-7.1s  Quieter still. A rack wheeled a short distance. The heavy clunk of an oven
          latch and a deep roll of escaping steam.
7.1-8.9s  THIS IS THE MOMENT THE WHOLE FILM IS BUILT AROUND. A loaf on a wire rack, its
          crust crackling and ticking as it cools — a fine, irregular, delicate series
          of snaps and pops, close-miked and clearly audible above everything else.
          Bring the rest of the mix down under it.
8.9-12.3s Kraft paper crumpling and folding, tape torn. Then outdoors: a freewheel
          ticking, tyres on wet tarmac, one blackbird, distant early traffic.
12.3-15s  The bag setting down on stone. Footsteps leaving. Birdsong. Then near silence
          under the end card, with one last faint crust tick.
No music, no voiceover, no narration, no dialogue, no whoosh transitions, no sound
effects that are not physically present in the scene.

[NEGATIVE]
No text of any kind other than the caption chips, the PROOF wordmark, and "now
delivering". No other logos, no brand names, no shop signage, no readable street signs,
no house numbers, no phone screens, no app interfaces, no price, no website, no social
handles, no hashtags, no watermarks. Do not alter the PROOF mark's ring count or the
spelling of the wordmark. No music. No people speaking. No faces held in close-up for
longer than one second — this is about the bread, not a character. No slow motion, no
speed ramps, no lens flare, no colour grading toward teal or blue. The caption chip
never moves position. Do not add a ninth shot. Do not animate the end card.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Lana Kane: a tall, striking brown-skinned female agent, wearing a stylish bikini. Exasperated, eyes-wide "are-you-kidding-me" energy.
~~~~

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Strict first-person POV, male hero, face never visible. Visible only the hero’s hands, fingers, and forearms when needed for casting, dodging balance, and the final magnetic...</summary>

~~~~text
Strict first-person POV, male hero, face never visible. Visible only the hero’s hands, fingers, and forearms when needed for casting, dodging balance, and the final magnetic strike. Broad male hands, pronounced knuckles, short nails, pale or cool-toned skin, dark gray or black sleeves, thin geometric magic lines glowing cold white with a faint blue tint across the skin and wrists. The hands move with force, precision, and short controlled gestures, never soft or decorative. One continuous shot, no cuts, vertical cinematic dark fantasy, realistic body-driven camera motion, professional action cinematography with physical momentum and readable combat beats.

In a vast abandoned stone quarry under a gray overcast sky, high rock walls, two dark mine tunnel arches in the background, old metal rails cutting through wet red-brown mud, overturned mine carts, scattered stones, chains, broken timber, iron scrap, discarded armor, and bodies of fallen guards. The space feels cold, damp, heavy, and dusty, with a steel-gray, dirty brown, graphite, and cold white palette. A squad of dark armored quarry guards charges first across the rails and muddy ground, helmets closed, carrying short spears, shields, and hammers. Behind them, emerging from the shadow of the mine arch, comes the boss: a giant one-eyed heavy warrior, 4 to 5 meters tall, rough gray-pink skin, one large round eye in the center of the face, partial metal armor and leather straps across the chest, a massive shoulder plate on one side, thick legs, immense weight, and a huge heavy battle axe carried with brutal momentum.

The hero briefly raises one hand holding a smooth semi-transparent magnetic orb, tennis-ball sized, silver-white with a pale blue core, a dense metallic vortex inside, thin rings of light rotating around it. The orb already exists when first shown and does not appear from nowhere. As the smaller guards rush in, the hero crushes the orb. A cold magnetic pulse flashes through both forearms and into the ground. Weapons rip out of the charging soldiers’ hands, shields twist sideways, bolts, chains, rail spikes, loose armor plates, and metal fragments shudder and begin lifting from the quarry floor. Some smaller enemies are violently disarmed and thrown off balance immediately.

The boss accelerates forward through the chaos and swings the giant axe in a heavy horizontal attack. The hero performs a fast professional sidestep and body lean at the last instant; the blade tears across the foreground with dangerous proximity, kicking mud and dust past the camera. The boss recovers with heavy inertia, plants a foot, and launches a second brutal overhead strike. The hero ducks and slips under it in a sharp Hollywood-style evasive motion, the axe crashing into the ground and rails with a shower of dirt, sparks, and metal shock. The camera reacts like a real head-and-torso dodge: fast lateral movement, recoil, breath, then immediate refocus on the boss.

Now the magnetic field fully takes hold. The hero spreads both hands outward. Metal rises from every direction in a controlled field, not chaotic: torn rails, chains, bolts, iron rings, armor plates, mine cart fragments, shield metal. The pieces lift, align, and begin orbiting the giant in three clear rotating bands: a lower ring around the knees, a middle ring around the waist and abdomen, and a top ring around the chest and shoulders. The boss tries to wrench the axe free and charge again, but the orbit tightens around him. Tight center framing, with strong tracking drift and a low-angle hold to emphasize mass and danger.

The hero then sharply pulls both hands inward. All three magnetic rings slam tighter at once. Chains snap taut, rails bend into crushing arcs, armor plates collide and lock, metal fragments hammer into the boss’s torso and arms. The giant loses balance, staggers, and is forced down, the axe slipping and dropping into the mud. The metal cocoon constricts harder with brutal industrial weight and magnetic pressure, locking his body in place. Dust rises in waves, small rocks skitter, chains strain, and the one eye flares with panic and rage.

The hero thrusts both hands forward for the finisher. A narrow concentrated magnetic impulse forms between the palms, not fire, but a dense electromagnetic strike with a bright white core and pale blue edges. Brief micro push-in to the center of the boss’s chest. The pulse hits. Every piece of metal bound around the giant clamps inward simultaneously with one final crushing lock. The boss drops fully to his knees, torso compressed under chained rails and armored fragments, while a deep metallic shock ripples through the quarry and a wave of dust spreads across the ground.

End on the giant kneeling and trapped in a brutal iron cocoon, chains stretched tight, rails embedded across the torso, the fallen axe half-buried in mud, drifting dust, and a few small metal fragments still hovering and spinning before they finally drop.

Lighting: cold gray cloud light, faint wet reflections on stone, mud, and rails, cold silver-white illumination from the orb and the hero’s magic lines, sharp cold highlights on airborne metal during orbit, and a final white-blue flash from the magnetic impulse. Cinematic dark fantasy, anamorphic look, subtle film grain, shallow depth of field on hands and nearby metal, strong physical inertia, realistic heavy enemy movement, rails resisting as they tear from the ground, chains tightening with believable tension, mud displacement, rolling debris, and controlled magnetic motion that feels like a field, not random telekinesis. Sound design: low wind in the quarry, distant metal groans, armor rattle from the charging guards, tight compression sound as the orb is crushed, deep low-frequency magnetic hum, metal shrieking free from the ground, heavy boss footsteps, axe whooshes with real mass, impact crashes into mud and rails, chain strain, rail collisions, a sharp electromagnetic finisher, then a massive iron-lock resonance and fading dust-heavy rumble.

No third-person view, no visible hero face, no constant hands in frame, no random floating metal chaos, no weak enemy scale, no weightless boss, no fire magic look, no glowing fantasy laser beam, no cartoon physics, no soft combat, no low-detail quarry, no tiny axe, no silly enemy behavior, no zombie movement.
~~~~

~~~~text
Build your storyboard, split it into scenes, and let it do the rest. ✨

Prompt ✨

REFERENCE USAGE — nine cropped storyboard panels, in order:
Image 1 — hook frame: the MiniMax H3 wordmark, the Create Beyond script line, the woman in violet light with jellyfish and particles.
Image 2 — multimodal frame: floating glass panels showing image, video and cosmic content, with its heading.
Image 3 — Hailuo frame: the MiniMax H3 x HailuoAI rounded glowing lockup on violet nebula.
Image 4 — editing frame: the floating glass editing timeline interface with its tool rail and clip thumbnails.
Image 5 — production frame: the woman with hair flying against violet speed streaks, with its heading.
Image 6 — live frame: the words LIVE RIGHT NOW in heavy white type over a violet horizon burst.
Image 7 — creators frame: the row of five vertical content cards and its heading.
Image 8 — possibilities frame: the four panel grid of marketing, film, music and games with its labels.
Image 9 — CTA frame: the MiniMax H3 x HailuoAI lockup with the AVAILABLE NOW glowing pill button.
Take every composition, subject, layout, wordmark and colour from these nine panels. Reproduce all typography, letterforms, weights, colours and positions exactly as they appear in the panels. Do not redraw, re-typeset, translate, correct or invent any text.

A 16:9 high energy sixty second style product launch commercial compressed into fifteen seconds, for an AI video generation platform. Palette locked to deep violet and near black with electric purple light, hot white type and bright particle energy. Every frame carries volumetric violet glow, drifting light particles, translucent glass surfaces with edge refraction, and soft bloom on every bright edge. Premium technology advertising in the language of a flagship device launch: confident, fast, expensive, futuristic. Never cheap, never cluttered, never retro.

Cutting: nine beats, hard cuts only, no dissolves or soft transitions. Hold every cut until its stated timecode and do not shorten any shot. Energy escalates continuously from the first frame to the last.

TEXT RENDERING: all on screen text is revealed by hard edged masks or lands complete on the cut, always fully sharp, at final size and full opacity, in its exact position. Never scale, fade, warp, letter space or motion blur any text. Never let text be soft, doubled or partially transparent at any point.

TEXT LOCK — only these strings appear, spelled exactly:
MiniMax H3 / Create Beyond. / Native Multimodal Generation / Text + Image + Video + Audio / Precise Multimodal Editing / Production-Ready Content Creation / For Creators. By Creators. / LIVE RIGHT NOW / Marketing / Film & TV / Music / Games / AVAILABLE NOW / HailuoAI
No numbers, captions, subtitles, credits or any other words appear anywhere.

[0-2s] Image 1. A violet shockwave detonates outward from frame centre through drifting particles and jellyfish, and the composition resolves out of it. The MiniMax H3 wordmark lands hard and complete. The Create Beyond script line writes on beneath it in one confident stroke as light traces the letterforms. HARD CUT.

[2-3.6s] Image 2. The glass panels fly in from depth and lock into their arrangement, each one settling with a soft refraction flare. The content inside each panel is live and moving. The heading lands complete above them. HARD CUT.

[3.6-4.8s] Image 3. The rounded lockup ignites, its glowing border drawing on around the corners in one continuous run of light. Jellyfish drift past in the violet nebula behind it. HARD CUT.

[4.8-6.4s] Image 4. The floating editing interface assembles in depth: the tool rail slides in from the left, the timeline extends to the right, clip thumbnails populate in sequence, and a selection highlight snaps onto one clip. The camera drifts subtly across the glass surface. HARD CUT.

[6.4-8.2s] Image 5. Full speed. Violet light streaks race past the lens at maximum velocity, her hair flying, particles tearing through frame. Camera [Push in] hard and fast. The heading lands mid motion. This is the peak of the film. HARD CUT.

[8.2-9.6s] Image 6. A burst of white light detonates on the horizon and LIVE RIGHT NOW lands enormous and complete, filling the frame. HARD CUT.

[9.6-11.2s] Image 7. The five vertical content cards fly in and lock into a row one after another in rapid succession, each landing with a violet edge flare, the content inside each card alive and moving. The heading lands above them. HARD CUT.

[11.2-12.8s] Image 8. The four panel grid snaps into place, each quadrant arriving on its own beat with its label landing complete. All four panels play live simultaneously. HARD CUT.

[12.8-15s] Image 9. Final lockup. Particles converge inward toward frame centre. The MiniMax H3 and HailuoAI marks land, then the AVAILABLE NOW pill ignites, its glowing border drawing around the button in one stroke and settling into a steady pulse. Everything holds still and sharp for the final second.

Score: an original high energy cinematic technology launch score drives the whole film. Instrumentation is a deep sub bass drone, hard synthetic percussion, rising synth arpeggios, wide detuned brass style stabs, granular risers and one enormous impact hit. Modern, confident, cinematic and expensive, in the language of a flagship product launch. No vocals, no lyrics, no orchestral strings, no retro synthwave.

The score opens at 0 seconds on one enormous impact hit and a low sub drone under the shockwave. From 2 seconds a hard percussive pulse enters and locks in, one hit per cut. From 4.8 seconds the arpeggios enter and begin climbing. From 6.4 seconds a granular riser builds hard under the speed streaks and peaks at 8.2 seconds, where the brass stabs and the full arrangement slam in at maximum weight for the LIVE RIGHT NOW frame. It drives at full power through 12.8 seconds. At 12.8 seconds everything resolves into one wide sustained chord over the final lockup, decaying gently to the end.

Sound design sits under the score: a deep whoosh and glass chime as each panel or card locks into place, a rising electrical charge as each glowing border draws on, a hard transient on every hard cut, and one clean synthetic confirmation tone as the AVAILABLE NOW button ignites. Keep all of it tight and low in the mix. No voiceover, no narrator, no dialogue, no library stock music. The score must be an original composition and must not imitate any existing track.

Do not add subtitles, captions, credits, watermarks, panel numbers, section labels or any word outside the TEXT LOCK list. Do not reproduce the storyboard grid, its white caption strips or its numbered headings. Do not alter any wordmark or logo. Do not change the woman's face between shots. No lens flare streaks across the whole frame, no glitch RGB split, no film grain, no letterboxing.
~~~~

~~~~text
Visual Style: Premium Hollywood blockbuster, photorealistic, cinematic, epic scale, realistic human anatomy, realistic dinosaur skin and movement, dramatic lighting, detailed environment, natural physics, intense facial expressions, high-end VFX, ARRI Alexa 35 look, anamorphic lens, shallow depth of field, HDR, subtle film grain, 4K cinematic quality, dynamic camera movement, realistic motion blur.

0–2 seconds — Establishing Shot:
Wide cinematic shot of an ancient jungle temple in the middle of a mysterious rainforest. The female warrior stands alone in the ruins, holding a large sword in one hand. Her clothes are rugged fantasy-warrior clothing, slightly wet from the rain. Mist moves through the trees while birds fly in the distant sky. Slow camera push-in toward her.

2–3 seconds — Hero Close-Up:
Cut to a dramatic close-up of the girl's face. Her hair is slightly wet and moving in the wind. She looks determined and fearless. Small scratches and dirt are visible on her face. She slowly raises her sword and prepares for battle.

3–4 seconds — Dinosaur Reveal:
Suddenly, a massive realistic T-Rex emerges from behind the jungle trees and roars aggressively. Its enormous jaws open, sharp teeth visible, water and mud splashing around its feet. Low-angle camera makes the dinosaur look huge and terrifying.

4–6 seconds — First Attack:
The dinosaur charges toward the girl at full speed. She runs toward the side and performs a fast dodge at the last second. The dinosaur crashes through trees and debris behind her. Use handheld camera movement, fast tracking, realistic impact and flying dust.

6–8 seconds — Sword Counterattack:
The girl quickly turns around and attacks with her sword. Show a dramatic slow-motion moment as she swings the sword toward the dinosaur. The dinosaur moves its head aggressively while roaring. Strong cinematic camera movement follows the sword.

8–10 seconds — Epic Jump:
The dinosaur attacks again. The girl jumps onto a large fallen tree and then leaps into the air above the dinosaur. Her hair and clothing move naturally in slow motion. Camera rotates around her as she raises the sword for the final strike.

10–12 seconds — Final Strike:
She lands a powerful cinematic sword strike against the dinosaur's armored hide. Create a dramatic impact with dust, rainwater, debris and strong camera shake. Keep the action non-gory and suitable for a blockbuster fantasy film.

12–13.5 seconds — Dinosaur Falls Back:
The dinosaur loses balance and falls backward into the muddy ground with a huge impact. Water, dust and leaves fly into the air. The girl lands safely and stands ready, breathing heavily.

13.5–14 seconds — Hero Ending:
Slow cinematic push-in toward the girl. She lowers the sword slightly and looks directly toward the camera with a powerful, fearless expression. Behind her, the defeated dinosaur lies in the misty jungle. Golden sunlight breaks through the clouds, creating an epic silhouette and emotional Hollywood-style ending.
Camera & Cinematography:
Wide establishing shots, low-angle dinosaur shots, close-up facial shots, handheld action camera, tracking shots, 360-degree movement during the jump, dramatic slow motion during key action moments, shallow depth of field for close-ups, realistic motion blur, cinematic composition.
Lighting & Atmosphere:
Dark stormy jungle atmosphere mixed with warm golden sunlight, volumetric light rays, wet surfaces, realistic rain, mist, smoke, flying leaves, dust and water particles.
~~~~
