~~~~text
Create a 15 second cinematic sports video in 16:9 inside a real indoor strength competition arena packed with spectators. The story contrasts repeated failure with one calm, unexpected success. It must feel like authentic live event footage captured by a professional broadcast crew, with natural human motion, believable weight, consistent anatomy, coherent crowd behavior, and physically accurate object movement.

0.00 to 2.70 seconds. Show three rapid failed keg toss attempts by three different heavyweight athletes. Use low sideline wide shots that include the athlete, tall steel throwing rig, red crossbar, and full keg arc. Each attempt lasts less than one second. One keg clips the bar and spins backward, one falls short, and one drifts sideways. The athletes show brief frustration while the crowd gives disappointed gasps. Use clean match cuts based on the circular keg shape.

2.70 to 4.40 seconds. Let the music soften. Cut to a medium wide shot of an unassuming compact middle aged competitor entering from the arena tunnel. He wears a plain black competition shirt, dark blue shorts, knee sleeves, lifting shoes, and a worn leather belt. He walks calmly toward the keg while larger athletes watch from the edge of frame.

4.40 to 7.20 seconds. Use three tactile close shots. Chalk pressing into his palms. Fingers locking around a cold dented steel rim. Shoes gripping the gray rubber floor as his knees bend. Show subtle breathing, sweat, fabric tension, skin compression, and a slight forearm tremor. Keep the same face, body, wardrobe, keg, and arena geography in every shot.

7.20 to 11.80 seconds. Continue in one unbroken low angle tracking shot with a natural 35 mm lens look. He swings the keg between his legs, drives through the hips, extends the knees, pulls upward, and releases at the correct moment. The camera makes a controlled upward tilt. The keg rotates with realistic inertia and follows a clean parabolic arc. Use natural motion blur at 30 frames per second with a 1 over 60 shutter feel. Hold a brief near silence before release.

11.80 to 13.40 seconds. The keg clears the red crossbar by only a few centimeters and lands heavily on the padded platform behind the rig without an unrealistic bounce. The crowd erupts on impact. A judge instinctively raises both arms to confirm success.

13.40 to 15.00 seconds. Cut to a medium close shot as the competitor raises one fist and gives a restrained relieved smile. Larger athletes behind him stare in disbelief, then applaud. Finish on a natural crowd reaction, not a freeze frame.

Use cool white overhead arena lighting, subtle blue haze, realistic warm skin tones, gentle lens bloom around practical lights, deep readable shadows, neutral cinematic color, controlled contrast, fine film grain, realistic sweat highlights, and no excessive sharpness.

Sound and music. Use an original 82 BPM cinematic percussion track with deep toms, restrained bass pulses, and a steady tension build. Layer authentic arena room tone, crowd murmur, chalk rubbing, metal scraping, shoe friction, breathing, a sharp air movement sound during release, a solid steel landing impact, and a powerful crowd swell after clearance. No dialogue.

Do not include text, captions, logos, watermarks, brand names, duplicated spectators, warped faces, extra fingers, changing clothing, inconsistent body size, floating objects, rubbery metal, impossible physics, sudden camera teleportation, or artificial slow motion. The final result must look photographic, grounded, spontaneous, and genuinely filmed at a live competition.
~~~~

</details>

The room is filled with broken ritual circles, ancient books, shattered altars, skulls, scattered candles, and dozens of lifeless witch bodies lying across the cold stone floor. Instead of wielding weapons, the ghost attacks using its razor-sharp claws and multiple arms, striking from impossible angles with incredible speed.

An intense close-quarters battle begins.

The warrior skillfully dodges the ghost's relentless claw attacks, parries with his enchanted sword, and cuts through several skeletal arms as they lunge toward him. The ghost continuously regenerates new arms, making the fight increasingly desperate.

The battle reaches its climax as the ghost charges forward with all of its arms at once. The warrior times the attack perfectly, leaps into the air, and delivers a single breathtaking spinning sword strike that cleanly severs the ghost's head.

The head falls to the stone floor as the ghost's body freezes in place, cracks with glowing purple energy, and disintegrates into countless glowing magical particles that fade into the air. The chamber becomes completely silent once again.

## 82. Been testing the new Flux3 and Hailuo Minimax H3 models like crazy lately.

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/been-testing-the-new-flux3-and-hailuo-minimax-h3-models-282651/video-94592e26e50f.webm">

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Prompt Duration: 20 seconds Aspect ratio: 21:9 Overview Photorealistic smartphone footage from a moving car. Begin directly behind a helmeted female rider, showing only her back....</summary>

~~~~text
Prompt

Duration: 20 seconds
Aspect ratio: 21:9

Overview
Photorealistic smartphone footage from a moving car. Begin directly behind a helmeted female rider, showing only her back. The filming car catches up and pulls alongside. She glances at the camera, then the motorcycle transforms into a two-seat hypercar. She removes her helmet and places the empty helmet on the passenger seat. In the final beat, the outer cover and canopy close while the car accelerates past the filming vehicle and ends ahead. One continuous shot.

Setting
Bright multilane highway, clear sky, sparse traffic, hard midday sun. Both vehicles move forward in parallel lanes. Road direction and lighting stay consistent.

Cast
[CHAR_A] Young woman in black riding gear. A graphite full-face helmet completely hides her face and hair. After removal she has one face and a blonde ponytail. Identity locked.

[VEH_A] Matte graphite futuristic motorcycle with blue-violet lights. Folded car structures are stored inside its chassis. Final form: low two-seat hypercar with four aligned wheels, passenger seat, dashboard, outer cover and transparent canopy.

Action
0–4s: Start strictly behind [CHAR_A], centered on her back, helmet and rear wheel. Her face and visor front are invisible. The filming car approaches faster from behind.

4–7s: The filming car reaches a rear three-quarter angle and pulls alongside. [CHAR_A] turns only her helmeted head, gives the camera a quick glance, then looks forward and continues straight.

7–12s: Transformation begins without slowing. Front and rear hubs split into paired wheel modules and slide outward on telescoping axles, forming four wheels. The frame widens, rails extend and folded panels rotate into wheel arches. The seat lowers into the driver cockpit. Every part stays connected through hinges, rails, gears, pistons and locks.

12–16s: The cockpit stays open. [CHAR_A] lifts the helmet vertically off her real head with both hands, revealing her single face and blonde ponytail. The removed helmet is an empty shell with only dark padding and empty space. Never show a face, eyes, skin, hair or duplicate head inside it. A passenger seat forms beside her. She places the helmet on it and it remains visible.

16–18s: Dashboard, controls, lower doors, wheel arches and rear panels lock into place. The upper cover and canopy remain open. All four tires stay on the road.

18–20s: [VEH_A] accelerates as the upper cover folds inward and the transparent canopy sweeps shut. Closing and overtaking happen simultaneously. While passing the filming car, the roof panels meet, the canopy seals and the final locks click. The hypercar pulls several lengths ahead. The camera pans forward slightly late. End with the fully closed car racing away in front.

Camera
Single continuous handheld smartphone shot from inside the moving passenger car. Begin behind the rider, then shift to rear three-quarter and side tracking only because the filming car catches up. No cuts, teleportation or impossible jumps. Natural motion blur and window reflections.

Physics
Fast, smooth mechanical transformation with believable weight and clearance. Panels never pass through the rider or helmet. Wheel rotation, suspension and road contact stay synchronized.

Audio
Highway wind, tires and traffic. Synchronized servos, rails, hydraulics and metal locks. Helmet handling sounds. During the final pass, electric turbine whine combines with cover-folding sounds and a strong sealing click. No dialogue or music.

Restrictions
Exactly one woman, one head, one face and one helmet. Face hidden until helmet removal. Removed helmet always empty. No duplicate face, disappearing helmet, floating parts, liquid morphing, clipping, extra wheels or malformed limbs. Exactly four wheels after transformation. The cover and canopy must close while it overtakes the filming car. End with the sealed car clearly ahead. No lane reversal, drift, crash, captions, logos, HUD or watermark.
~~~~

</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Minimax prompt [1] FORMAT 15-second 16:9 stylized anime action title sequence — martial-arts choreography fused with 3D kinetic typography. Painterly 2D anime illustration look...</summary>

~~~~text
Minimax prompt

[1] FORMAT
15-second 16:9 stylized anime action title sequence — martial-arts choreography fused with 3D kinetic typography. Painterly 2D anime illustration look with cel-shaded volumes and subtle paper grain, matching the source artwork's rendering.

[2] ASSETS
Use Image 1 as a strict identity reference for the female duelist KAELAN — read only the character artwork itself for her face, hair, wardrobe, weapon, and color palette. Ignore the sheet's layout, panels, printed labels, handwriting, silhouette thumbnails, and background parchment; none of them may appear in the video.

[3] IDENTITY
Preserve exactly: deep brown skin with warm highlights; luminous crimson-red eyes with a sharp black liner shape; jet-black hair pulled into a tight scalp-wrapped part that splits into two very long thick braids falling past the hips, moving like weighted whips; large white crescent hoop earrings on both ears; black high-neck sleeveless crop top exposing a toned midriff; a sheer white long-sleeved kimono robe worn open, translucent and lightly billowing; cream wide-leg hakama trousers with a single black diamond patch on the left thigh; black wrapped fingerless gloves with lattice cord across the back of the hand; white cloth-wrapped platform sandals; a well-worn katana with a dark blue-grey blade and a black diamond-wrapped hilt. Her expression stays calm and unblinking, never comic.

[4] BEATS
Shot 1 — [0–3 seconds] Kaelan stands centered in a void of warm off-white light, katana point-down, braids settling. She exhales; a slow iai draw begins. → Hard cut.
Shot 2 — [3–6 seconds] Mid-draw. The blade clears the saya and a clean arc of light follows the edge. The 3D extruded word "HAILUO" flies in from screen left along the blade's arc, its letter faces catching the same light, passing BEHIND her shoulder and IN FRONT of her trailing braid before exiting right. → Whip-pan cut.
Shot 3 — [6–9 seconds] Full-body kata combination: descending cut, pivot, rising horizontal sweep. Her sheer robe and both braids trail one frame behind her body. The word "MINIMAX" rotates up from the floor plane in thick extruded 3D, standing taller than her, and she cuts through the gap between its letters — the letterforms hold solid, do not shatter. → Hard cut.
Shot 4 — [9–12.5 seconds] Low-angle crouch landing, sword arm extended back, dust ring pushing outward. "H3" slams in from depth as a massive chromed 3D glyph directly behind her, scaling down to lock, with her silhouette reading cleanly against it. → Flash cut on the impact frame.
Shot 5 — [12.5–15 seconds] She rises into the neutral standing pose, katana returning to point-down. The full lockup "HAILUO MiniMax H3" assembles from the three pieces on a single horizontal baseline at lower third, settles, and holds perfectly still through the final frame.

[5] CAMERA
Anamorphic-feel 35mm for wides, 50mm for the mid shots.
~~~~

</details>

Important rules:
- The girl is always framed in close-up or medium shots only. No wide or distant shots at all.
- The girl performs continuously for the full 10 seconds without any cuts on her. Her movement is natural and fluid — she can shift weight, make small dance-like gestures, turn her head, move her shoulders and hands, but the camera stays on her in consistent medium/close framing.
- Typography and background change with hard cuts according to the storyboard order, while the girl keeps performing through them.

Sequence of text (hard cuts):
1. “THE TABLE’S SET AGAIN”
2. “NOBODY SITS DOWN”
3. “YOUR WORDS ARE HARD GLASS”
4. “FALLING TO THE FLOOR NOW”
5. “I KEPT THE DOOR OPEN”
6. “YOU KEPT YOUR HANDS CROSSED”
7. “SAME ROOM, SAME SCAR”
8. “SAME OLD COST”
9. Final rose  The girl sings the entire song with clear, accurate lip-sync.

Mouth and teeth must stay natural and undistorted at all times — no deformation, no melting, no artifacts. Emotional facial performance. Typography stays sharp and reacts (cracks, shifts, particles). Changing typography every 1–1.5 seconds. Heavy red atmosphere, cinematic lighting, photoreal

## 87. 動画プロンプトはリプ欄で

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/video-prompt-case-098132-098132/video-ede22d0cb4bb.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/video-prompt-case-098132-098132/poster-134bd905899f.jpg" alt="動画プロンプトはリプ欄で video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — タイトル｜SUMMER CONTACT SHEET サブタイトル｜KINETIC PHOTO MONTAGE 尺｜15秒 画角｜9:16 解像度｜1080×1920 FPS｜24 ジャンル｜高速フォトモンタージュ／サマーファッション広告／日本語手書きタイポグラフィ／モーショングラフィックス...</summary>

~~~~text
タイトル｜SUMMER CONTACT SHEET サブタイトル｜KINETIC PHOTO MONTAGE 尺｜15秒 画角｜9:16 解像度｜1080×1920 FPS｜24 ジャンル｜高速フォトモンタージュ／サマーファッション広告／日本語手書きタイポグラフィ／モーショングラフィックス コンセプト｜海辺で過ごした夏の一日を、縦長写真を中心にした高速フォトモンタージュで振り返る。写真をマス目状に並べず、1枚の大きな写真へ次の写真が右、左、上、下、斜めから差し込まれ、前の写真を押し出しながら連鎖する。画面内へ同時に表示する写真は基本1〜3枚。約22〜24枚の夏の断片を高速で見せ、最後だけ切り替えを止める。選ばれた一枚の縦写真が9:16全画面へ広がり、手書き文字で「この一枚が、夏になる。」と書いて締める。 主人公設定｜実写風の成人女性。健康的で自然な美しさ。未成年に見えない。白とコバルトブルーを基調にした洗練された水着。グラビアではなく爽やかなサマーファッション広告の印象。髪はミディアム〜ロングで海風になびく。全写真で同じ顔、髪型、体型、水着、配色を維持する。自然な笑顔、振り向き、歩く、走る、水に触れる、空を見る、砂浜で休むなど、夏の一日が伝わる動作を使う。 写真設計｜縦長9:16を主役フォーマットとし、横長16:9、正方形1:1、細い横帯、細い縦帯をアクセントとして使用する。主役写真は常に画面の60〜100％を占める。小写真を大量に並べず、大きな写真を残したまま次の写真を重ねる。写真には白またはオフホワイトの細い縁、軽いフィルムグレイン、フィルム番号、赤・青・黄色の手書きマーカーを加える。 写真内容｜後ろ姿で海を見る／振り向く笑顔／風になびく髪／波に触れる足元／水を蹴る／走る全身／水しぶき越しの顔／サングラスを持つ手／冷たいドリンク／砂浜の足跡／青空と雲／海面の反射／砂へ触れる手／座って海を見る／寝転んで空を見る／遠くを見る横顔／タオルや羽織りを持つ／夕方のシルエットなど。人物、風景、手元、足元、ディテールを交互に見せる。 色設計｜コバルトブルー、シアン、白、オフホワイトを基本とし、手書き文字とマーカーにレモンイエロー、朱赤、濃いブルーを使用する。序盤は青と白を中心に静かに始め、中盤で黄色と赤を増やし、終盤は柔らかな午後の光へ変化する。 文字設計｜途中の短い手書き文字は「風」「ひかり」「いま」「まぶしい」「自由」「波」。写真の余白や境界線に短時間だけ表示し、写真と一緒に移動・押し出し・消失させる。画面下へ字幕として固定しない。最終コピーは「この一枚が、夏になる。」。最後の写真上へ、実際にマーカーで書くように一画ずつ表示する。 使用写真数｜約22〜24枚。CUT01＝3枚、CUT02＝5枚、CUT03＝7〜8枚、CUT04＝7〜8枚、CUT05＝最終写真1枚。同時表示は最大3枚程度。全写真を一覧表示しない。 CUT01｜0:00〜0:02.5｜Visuals: 画面中央に、海を見つめる主人公の後ろ姿を写した縦長9:16写真が一枚だけ全画面表示される。写真内の髪と波が0.3秒だけ動き、シャッター音で静止。0:00.8で右から振り向く顔の縦写真が入り、最初の写真を左へ約35％押し出す。0:01.5で下から波に触れる足元の横長写真がせり上がり、画面下部を覆う。0:02.1で足元写真が上へ抜け、後ろ姿写真が再び中央へ戻る。｜Camera: 後ろ姿35mm・F4.5・緩やかな前進、顔70mm・F2.8、足元24mm・F5.0・ローアングル。｜Motion: 右→左、下→上の写真スライド。写真内の動きは0.2〜0.4秒で止める。｜Lighting: 柔らかな夏の自然光、海面のきらめき、人物を暗くしない軽い逆光。｜Typography: 「風」を細い白の手書き文字で顔写真の髪の流れに沿って表示。顔写真と一緒に左へ押し出されて消える。｜VFX: 細い白枠、フィルム番号01〜03、軽い粒子、青い小さな矢印。｜SFX: シャッター3回、波、風、写真の紙擦れ音。｜Emotion: 夏の入口、静かな期待感。｜Transition: 足元の白い水しぶきが下から画面全体へ広がり、次の写真へ白マスクで切り替える。 CUT02｜0:02.5〜0:05.0｜Visuals: 白い水しぶきの中から主人公の笑顔アップが全画面へ出現。0:02.9で左から髪が風になびく細い縦帯写真が差し込み、顔写真を右へ押す。0:03.3で上から青空と雲の横長写真が降り、画面上部を約35％覆う。0:03.7で右下からサングラスを持つ手元の正方形写真が斜めに滑り込む。0:04.1で中央から海面の反射写真が拡大して前の写真を覆う。0:04.6で走り出す主人公の縦写真が右から入り、次カットへつなぐ。｜Camera: 顔85mm・F2.8、髪100mm・F2.5、空24mm・F5.6、手元50mm・F3.5、海面100mm・F4.0、全身35mm・F4.0。｜Motion: 左右の押し出し、上からの落下、右下からの斜め進入、中央拡大を連続。1写真0.3〜0.5秒。｜Lighting: 真夏の強い光へ変化。髪、肩、海面へ明るいハイライト。｜Typography: 海面写真に黄色の「ひかり」、走る写真の右上へ青い「いま」。文字は写真と同時に進入し、次の写真に押し出される。｜VFX: 黄色い手描き線、白い丸囲み、赤い小矢印、写真境界の軽い影。｜SFX: シャッター連写、キラッ、写真スライド、風。｜Emotion: 夏が一気に動き出す高揚感。｜Transition: 走る主人公が画面下から上へ駆け抜け、その動線に沿って次の写真が下からせり上がる。 CUT03｜0:05.0〜0:08.0｜Visuals: 最も高速なフォトモンタージュ。0:05.0走る全身の縦写真、0:05.35下から水を蹴る横長写真、0:05.7左から水しぶき越しの笑顔、0:06.05右上から冷たいドリンクの手元、0:06.4斜め境界で砂浜の足跡、0:06.75下から海を見上げる横顔、0:07.1右からサングラス越しの目元、0:07.45中央から両手を広げる全身写真が急拡大する。画面内には常に1〜3枚だけ残し、前写真を完全に消す前に次写真が一瞬だけ重なる。｜Camera: 全身24mm、足元20mm、水しぶき35mm、手元70mm、足跡35mm、横顔85mm、目元100mm、全身28mm。｜Motion: 下→上、左→右、右上→左下、中央拡大など進入方向を毎回変える。主人公の視線、手足、水しぶきの方向を次写真へつなぐマッチカットを使う。｜Lighting: 真夏のハードライト。水滴、氷、肌、髪の輪郭が鮮明に光る。｜Typography: 水しぶき写真に白抜きの「まぶしい」、最後の全身写真の背景へ巨大な青文字「自由」。文字は人物を隠さず、一部が写真枠の外へはみ出す。｜VFX: 青と黄色の色帯、ハーフトーン、赤いマーカー丸、手描き矢印。｜SFX: 高速シャッター、水音、氷音、シュッという写真移動音、軽いポップ音。｜Emotion: 夏のピーク、自由な開放感。｜Transition: 両手を広げる写真へ急接近し、水着の白い部分をマスクとして次カットへ切り替える。 CUT04｜0:08.0〜0:12.0｜Visuals: 高速さを残しながら、夏の時間を昼から夕方へ進める。0:08.0砂浜に座って海を見る縦写真、0:08.5右から波に触れる手元写真、0:09.0上から空と雲の横長写真、0:09.45左下から寝転んで空を見る写真、0:09.9右から笑いながら振り向く顔、0:10.4下からタオルを肩にかけた上半身、0:10.9左から午後の柔らかな横顔、0:11.4上下分割で夕方の海と主人公のシルエット。前の写真の一部を画面端に残しながら次へ切り替え、時間と記憶が積み重なる感覚を出す。｜Camera: 座る姿50mm、手元70mm、空24mm、寝転ぶ姿35mm、顔50mm、上半身50mm、横顔85mm、シルエット70mm。｜Motion: 前半は0.4〜0.5秒、後半は0.5〜0.7秒へ徐々に減速。最後の夕方写真が縦方向へゆっくり全画面化する。｜Lighting: 白い昼光から金色の午後光へ変化。夕方写真には柔らかなリムライト。｜Typography: 手元写真に「波」、午後の横顔に「ひかり」、夕方写真の余白へ「いま」を表示。「いま」だけ次カット直前まで少し長く残す。｜VFX: 赤いチェック、白い手描き円、フィルム番号、写真枠の柔らかな影。｜SFX: シャッター間隔が徐々に広がり、波と風が前へ出る。BGMの音数も減らす。｜Emotion: 楽しかった一日が記憶へ変化する余韻。｜Transition: 夕方写真中央に小さな白い写真枠が現れ、その枠がゆっくり拡大して最後の一枚へ変わる。 CUT05｜0:12.0〜0:15.0｜Visuals: 写真の切り替えが完全に止まり、主人公の自然な笑顔、海風になびく髪、輝く海、柔らかな午後の光が写った縦長9:16写真が全画面へ広がる。偶然撮れた忘れられない一枚のような自然さを持たせる。0:12.4から白または赤のマーカーで「この一枚が、夏になる。」を一画ずつ書き始め、0:14.3で句点を書く。小さく「SUMMER CONTACT SHEET」を補助タイトルとして入れてもよい。｜Camera: 50mm・F3.2。写真が全画面化したあと、2〜3％だけゆっくり前進。文字完成時に停止。｜Motion: 最初の0.5秒だけ髪と波がわずかに動き、その後写真として静止。文字は手書きストロークで現れ、句点で小さくインクがにじむ。｜Lighting: 柔らかな午後の光。顔、髪、海を明るく見せ、自然な広告写真として整える。｜Typography: 「この一枚が、夏になる。」。読みやすい手書きマーカー文字。顔や身体へ重ならないよう、空または海の余白部分へ縦書きと横書きを組み合わせて配置してもよい。｜VFX: 軽いフィルムグレイン、微細な印刷ムラ。ラストではハーフトーンや色帯を減らし、写真と文字を主役にする。｜SFX: マーカー筆記音、句点で最後のシャッター音、波と風だけを残す。｜Emotion: 流れていった多くの瞬間から、一枚が夏の記憶として残る。｜Transition: 一枚の写真として静止終了。 編集テンポ｜CUT01は0.7〜1秒単位。CUT02は0.3〜0.5秒単位。CUT03は0.3〜0.4秒単位。CUT04は0.4〜0.7秒単位から徐々に減速。CUT05は一枚を3秒間見せる。高速パートと静止パートの差を明確にする。 カメラ・レイアウトルール｜完成画角は9:16。縦写真を主役として大きく使う。左右からの写真は主役を30〜50％押し出し、上下写真は画面の30〜45％を一時的に覆う。斜め進入も使用するが、常に人物の顔や視線が見えるようにする。同時表示は最大3枚。均等分割、整列グリッド、写真一覧は禁止。 BGM｜爽やかで少しエモい夏のポップインスト。0:00〜0:05は軽いリズム、0:05〜0:08でテンポと音数を最大化、0:08〜0:12で少しずつ減速、0:12以降はメロディーと環境音を残して着地する。 必須条件｜完成動画9:16／約22〜24枚の夏写真を高速で紹介／写真一覧を表示しない／常時1〜3枚／右・左・上・下・斜めから写真が進入／写真だけで夏の一日が伝わる／途中に短い日本語手書きタイポ／最後は一枚の縦写真へ収束／「この一枚が、夏になる。」を手書きで表示／同一人物と同じ水着を全写真で維持する。 禁止事項｜写真グリッド／均等なマス目／全写真の同時表示／小さな写真を大量に並べる／常時4枚以上の画面分割／同じ顔写真だけの連続／字幕帯／意味不明な長文文字／暗すぎる写真／激しいグリッチ／未成年に見える人物／過度に性的なポーズ／人物の別人化。
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
7-second video. The Office (US) mockumentary style, inside the Dunder Mifflin conference room in Scranton. Harsh fluorescent office lighting, beige walls, cheap chairs, whiteboard, muted colors, slightly soft consumer-documentary camera quality. Michael Scott stands at the head of the table while Jim, Pam, Dwight, and Stanley sit around him. Handheld single-camera cinematography with subtle shake, sudden reframing, and fast reaction zooms.

Michael raises one hand with complete sincerity, defending an absurd opinion.

MICHAEL: “Everybody says Donald Trump’s fart smelled terrible. I was there. Honestly? Not that bad.”

Instant snap zoom to Dwight, who nods gravely.

DWIGHT: “Strong. Presidential.”

Jim slowly turns toward the documentary camera with a silent, deeply uncomfortable stare. Pam presses her lips together, trying not to laugh. Stanley keeps reading his crossword without looking up.

Natural English dialogue, precise lip synchronization, realistic overlapping office ambience, awkward silence after the punchline, no laugh track, no subtitles, no on-screen text, dry deadpan comedy, authentic mockumentary performances.
~~~~

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action, cinematic, photorealistic with true fine pores and real skin texture, no beauty filter. A wide low-angle shot in a wrecked...</summary>

~~~~text
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Live-action, cinematic, photorealistic with true fine pores and real skin texture, no beauty filter. A wide low-angle shot in a wrecked living room at dusk: a shattered window, an overturned wooden chair, a smashed vase with white petals scattered across the floor, broken glass everywhere and dust hanging in the low amber light. A 25-year-old woman with cool fair skin, dark almond eyes, long straight black hair and a plain charcoal slip dress stands motionless in the centre of the wreckage, arms at her sides, looking straight into the lens. The camera performs an Arc Shot with small amplitude at slow speed, circling her from left to right for the whole shot and never changing direction. At 00:03.000 the destruction begins to run backwards: every object except the woman moves as if the footage were playing in reverse, while she stays in normal forward time and does not move at all. Broken glass lifts off the floor, rises through the air and knits itself back into the window pane from the outside in. The white petals float upward, gather and fold back into a whole vase that lands upright on the table. The overturned chair swings up onto its legs. Dust and small fragments are drawn inward from every corner of the room, and a pool of water on the floor pulls back up into an intact drinking glass. At 00:09.000 the last few fragments converge on the window. At 00:11.000 the final shard slots into place and the room is completely whole and undamaged, warm evening light now falling through clean glass.

[Shot 2] At 00:11.500, the camera cuts to a chest-up close-up, Static Shot at eye level, of the same woman in the now-perfect room, her appearance, hair and charcoal dress unchanged. She is still motionless. She blinks once, the only human movement in the film, and the frame holds on her face until the end. No on-screen text or subtitles at any point.

overall_soundscape: Deep silence in the wrecked room, broken only by the woman's slow breathing. As the reversal starts, glass shards tick and chime upward through the air and wood scrapes as the chair rights itself, under a long inward rushing sound that draws the dust back from the edges of the room. The final shard clicks into the window and everything stops into complete silence.

non_diegetic_music: A single low synth drone that begins under the wreckage and rises steadily in pitch and volume through the reassembly, joined by sparse reversed piano notes. It cuts off dead at the moment the last shard locks into the window, leaving nothing underneath.
~~~~

</details>

## 90. Hailuo MiniMax H3 vs. Seedance 2.0

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/hailuo-minimax-h3-vs-seedance-2-0-186452/video-4049eb1f4eaf.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/hailuo-minimax-h3-vs-seedance-2-0-186452/poster-2173025914f5.jpg" alt="Hailuo MiniMax H3 vs. Seedance 2.0 video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a 15-second photorealistic cinematic dark-fantasy sequence in 16:9 widescreen. The video must contain exactly three clearly separated cinematic shots connected by clean...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a 15-second photorealistic cinematic dark-fantasy sequence in 16:9 widescreen. The video must contain exactly three clearly separated cinematic shots connected by clean hard cuts. No dissolves, morphing transitions or continuous camera orbit.

MASTER DRAGON LOCK:
Show one enormous ancient dragon with a broad powerful body, charcoal-black overlapping scales, subtle dark crimson colouring across its head and neck, two swept-back horns, smaller facial spikes, glowing amber-orange eyes, one long tail and one pair of enormous bat-like wings with dark leathery membranes. The dragon has four legs: two forelegs and two hind legs. Its design, size, horns, colour, anatomy and facial structure remain identical in all three shots. The dragon must feel extremely heavy, ancient and physically realistic.

MASTER RIDER LOCK:
One adult armoured rider is already seated securely in a dark leather saddle on the dragon from the very first frame. The rider wears detailed weathered silver plate armour, dark under-clothing, a fitted medieval helmet with a narrow shadowed visor and a long dark cloak. The rider’s face is never clearly visible. The rider holds leather reins connected to the saddle and remains seated throughout the entire sequence. The rider does not suddenly appear, mount, dismount or change clothing. During the first shot, the rider is naturally hidden behind the dragon’s large head, neck and folded wings.

ENVIRONMENT AND LIGHTING:
The sequence takes place inside a gigantic ancient mountain cave. The cave contains rough black rock walls, enormous stone formations, drifting mist, scattered bones and faint glowing embers between cracks in the ground. The far interior is almost black, but the dragon must remain readable through soft amber reflections and cold blue rim light entering from the cave mouth. The cave entrance opens from a high mountain cliff into a vast dramatic landscape of jagged mountains, deep valleys, distant waterfalls, rolling clouds and pale stormy daylight. Maintain a cinematic cold-blue and muted-amber colour palette with deep shadows, volumetric light rays and realistic atmospheric haze. Dark and mysterious, but never so underexposed that the dragon or rider becomes blurry or invisible.

SHOT 1 — DRAGON EMERGES, 0–5 SECONDS:
Begin with a symmetrical low frontal wide shot positioned near the cave entrance, facing deep into the darkness. The camera slowly tracks backward toward the entrance as the enormous dragon walks forward out of the black cave interior. The dragon’s wings remain completely folded against both sides of its body. Its glowing amber eyes appear first, followed by its horned head, shoulders and powerful forelegs becoming visible through the mist.

Each heavy footstep shakes the cave floor and dislodges small stones and dust from the ceiling. The dragon exhales dense vapour from its nostrils and releases a deep restrained growl. Its head moves naturally with the weight of each step. The dragon looks directly past the camera toward the cave opening. The rider is already seated but remains hidden behind the dragon’s head and neck. Do not reveal the rider clearly during this shot.

SHOT 2 — RIDER REVEAL, 5–9 SECONDS:
Clean hard cut to a low three-quarter side tracking shot beside the moving dragon. Begin close to the dragon’s horned head and sharply detailed scales. Track smoothly backward along the dragon’s neck and folded wing while the dragon continues walking toward the cave entrance.

As the camera reaches the dragon’s shoulder, reveal the single armoured rider seated in the saddle between the folded wings. Hold the composition long enough to clearly show the rider’s silver armour, helmet, dark cloak and both hands gripping the reins. The rider looks toward the bright cave exit and leans slightly forward in preparation for takeoff. The dragon and rider remain in continuous forward motion. Do not show a separate rider anywhere else in the cave.

SHOT 3 — REAR TAKEOFF AND FLIGHT, 9–15 SECONDS:
Clean hard cut to an epic wide rear view positioned behind and slightly above the dragon and rider. The bright cave mouth and vast mountain landscape fill the background ahead of them. The dragon accelerates into a powerful run toward the cave opening while its wings remain folded enough to pass safely through the cave.

The dragon reaches the cliff edge and makes one forceful leap completely out of the cave. Only after clearing the rock opening, it spreads both enormous wings to their full symmetrical width. The sudden wing expansion pushes mist, dust and loose stones outward from the cave entrance. The rider leans forward, grips the reins and remains firmly attached to the saddle.

The dragon performs one enormous downward wingbeat, catches the air and transitions naturally into stable forward flight. Loose stones fall from the cliff beneath it. The camera rapidly follows from behind as the dragon and rider soar away over the deep valley between jagged mountain peaks. The long tail streams naturally behind the dragon and the rider’s cloak whips in the wind. End on a majestic wide rear silhouette of the dragon flying into the open mountain landscape as sunlight breaks through the storm clouds.

CAMERA AND MOTION:
Premium cinematic camera movement with realistic scale and parallax. Shot one is a controlled backward dolly. Shot two is a smooth low side-tracking reveal. Shot three is a dynamic rear pursuit shot that follows the takeoff out of the cave. Use natural motion blur only during the leap and wingbeat. Keep the dragon’s movement heavy on the ground and powerful but graceful in the air. No shaky chaotic framing and no extreme close-ups that obscure the action.

CINEMATIC QUALITY:
Big-budget photorealistic dark-fantasy visual effects, highly detailed scales and armour, realistic creature muscles beneath the skin, physically accurate wing membranes, believable foot contact, convincing dust and falling-rock physics, volumetric cave mist, dramatic rim lighting, strong depth, atmospheric perspective and stable character consistency.

AUDIO:
No dialogue and no music. Deep dragon breathing, restrained growl, heavy echoing footsteps, claws scraping stone, armour movement, leather saddle creaking, falling pebbles, rushing cave wind, powerful wingbeat and a deep dragon roar as it enters open air.

STRICT CONSISTENCY:
Exactly one dragon and one rider. The rider is seated on the dragon from the beginning and never disappears. No additional people, dragons or creatures. No dragon transformation or colour change. No extra heads, horns, tails, wings, legs or riders. The wings remain folded inside the cave and open only after the dragon has completely cleared the cave mouth. The wings must not clip through the cave walls. The rider must not float above the saddle or merge into the dragon. No fire breathing, combat, dialogue, text, logos, blood or visible injuries.

## 91. Made it on

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/made-it-on-225758/video-67e141c9ffd3.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/made-it-on-225758/poster-905c4eb5b416.jpg" alt="Made it on video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a 15-second photorealistic cinematic dark-fantasy battlefield sequence in 16:9 widescreen. The video contains exactly two shots connected by one clean hard cut at...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a 15-second photorealistic cinematic dark-fantasy battlefield sequence in 16:9 widescreen. The video contains exactly two shots connected by one clean hard cut at approximately 11 seconds. No dissolves, morphing transitions or montage editing.

MASTER CREATURE LOCK:
Show exactly one colossal ash-white bat-dragon with wyvern anatomy. The creature has one broad skull-like head, two glowing ember-red eyes, one enormous mouth filled with long dark teeth, pale hairless scaled skin, a powerful chest, two muscular hind legs, one long narrow tail and one pair of gigantic bat-like wings. Its wings have strong finger-like bones supporting huge semi-translucent pale membranes. The wings glow warm orange when backlit by the battlefield fires.

The bat-dragon has no separate forelegs or human arms. Its wings are its only front limbs. Its design, colour, face, size, anatomy and wing structure remain identical throughout both shots. It never transforms, multiplies, changes colour or grows additional limbs.

BATTLEFIELD AND ARMY:
A vast apocalyptic fantasy battlefield beneath turbulent charcoal-grey storm clouds. The ground is dark, broken and covered with ash, embers, scorched rocks and drifting smoke. Large walls of orange fire burn across the distant horizon, strongly backlighting the approaching bat-dragon.

Hundreds of shadowy horned warriors crowd the battlefield. The warriors wear dark rough armour and simple horned helmets. Keep the warriors anonymous, visually similar and mostly seen as small silhouettes. No individual warrior is a main character and no faces require close detail. The army fills the foreground and middle distance to establish the impossible scale of the flying creature.

Use a cold grey-blue environment contrasted with intense orange firelight, glowing embers and warm translucent wing membranes. The environment must remain clearly visible through the smoke.

SHOT 1 — COLOSSAL LOW FLYOVER, 0–11 SECONDS:
Begin with a low ground-level ultra-wide camera positioned inside the tightly packed horned army, looking toward the burning horizon. Foreground warriors partially frame the bottom and sides of the image. The handheld camera moves slightly between the crowd while always looking forward.

Through the smoke and fire, the silhouette of one colossal bat-dragon appears in the sky. Its enormous folded wing shape initially resembles two mountains rising through the flames. The dragon then opens both wings to their full symmetrical width, completely dominating the sky. Bright orange fire shines through the pale wing membranes while the creature’s red eyes become visible.

The dragon performs one slow, immensely powerful downward wingbeat and descends toward the battlefield. The wingbeat pushes smoke outward in a huge circular pattern. The army looks upward and begins moving in panic as the creature rapidly approaches.

The bat-dragon glides extremely low above the battlefield without landing. Its chest and head pass just above the horned warriors. It banks slightly, bringing one enormous wing close over the foreground camera while the other wing remains fully visible and correctly attached. The wing itself does not physically strike the warriors.

The pressure wave created by the low flyover blasts across the army. Ash, embers, loose weapons, cloth and small pieces of debris surge sideways through the frame. Foreground warriors are knocked off balance, lifted briefly and scattered by the powerful wind, tumbling away from the creature without graphic impact or injury. Keep the movement chaotic but physically believable.

The camera shakes violently from the wing pressure and quickly pans to follow the dragon’s low flight across the battlefield. Flames bend beneath the airflow and smoke rolls outward behind the creature. Maintain a clear view of the same bat-dragon throughout the flyover. The dragon must not disappear completely behind its own wing or the smoke.

SHOT 2 — FRONTAL ROARING CHARGE, 11–15 SECONDS:
At approximately 11 seconds, make one clean hard cut to a frontal low-angle telephoto shot directly in the bat-dragon’s flight path. The creature is already flying forward through thick smoke and towering flames, coming straight toward the camera.

Keep both enormous wings visible on either side of its body as they perform one powerful coordinated wingbeat. The glowing red eyes remain locked directly on the camera. Burning embers stream around the creature while the orange flames behind it illuminate the translucent wing membranes.

As the bat-dragon rapidly closes the distance, it lowers its skull-like head and opens its enormous mouth, revealing multiple rows of long sharp teeth. It releases a deafening roar directly toward the camera. The force of the roar pushes smoke and embers outward from its face.

The camera retreats slightly while the dragon continues approaching, creating intense forward momentum. Finish just before impact with the creature’s roaring face, glowing eyes and wings dominating the frame. The dragon does not hit or pass through the camera.

CAMERA AND MOTION:
Shot one uses a low handheld battlefield perspective with strong scale, foreground silhouettes, realistic parallax and a fast pan following the flyover. Shot two uses a steady frontal tracking camera that retreats as the dragon approaches. Use cinematic motion blur on the wing tips, airborne ash and scattered debris, but keep the creature’s face and body sharply readable. The motion must feel fast, heavy and dangerous rather than weightless.

VISUAL QUALITY:
Premium big-budget dark-fantasy visual effects, photorealistic creature texture, physically accurate wing membranes, believable muscles moving beneath pale skin, enormous scale, detailed fire and smoke simulations, dense volumetric atmosphere, realistic aerodynamic pressure, strong depth, natural motion blur, sharp creature details and dramatic fire backlighting.

AUDIO:
No dialogue and no music. Roaring battlefield fire, panicked distant army sounds, rushing wind, deep leathery wingbeats, armour rattling, debris scraping across the ground, heavy low-frequency pressure wave and one terrifying creature roar during the final frontal approach.

STRICT CONSISTENCY:
Exactly one bat-dragon. No additional flying creatures. No rider. Exactly one head, two eyes, one mouth, two wings, two hind legs and one tail. No extra wings, arms, heads, faces or tails. The wings must remain attached to the same creature and must not turn into hands. The dragon never lands, breathes fire or changes design. Keep both wings intact and symmetrical. The horned warriors remain separate from the dragon and do not merge into its body. No visible casualties, blood, gore, text, subtitles, logos or real-world symbols.
~~~~

</details>

15 seconds, 16:9 dark-pop music video performance. Use @Image 1 as the strict identity reference for the three women — faces, hair and wardrobe unchanged in every shot. Use @Image 2 as the reference for the two titles only: ultra-condensed heavy caps, bone white, photocopied print surface.  The three women perform and sing to camera throughout. Follow the beats below shot by shot with continuous natural camera movement and real physical motion inside every frame — this is live performance footage, never a slideshow, never a sequence of held poses.  VOCAL: a single female lead voice, gritty and low, close-mic'd with tape saturation. The lyric lines are: "Dead air on the line" — sung at 2.7 seconds "Run it till it burns" — sung at 6.0 seconds "I don't slow down" — sung at 10.0 seconds Lip-sync locks precisely to these lines on whichever woman is singing. Between the lines all three mouth along, breathe hard and move.  [0.0–2.7s] The three stand in near-blackness, sand-beige studio crushed dark. Handheld camera drifting slowly forward, breathing. The platinum-crop woman rolls her shoulders and steps toward lens. Her hair lifts. The auburn woman in mirrored shades tilts her head up. Real movement, low energy, building. Title over the darkness: "DEAD AIR".  [2.7–6.0s] The auburn woman in the mirrored shades sings the first line straight into the lens, medium-close, mouth clearly forming the words. Camera whip-pans off her to the platinum-crop woman mid-stride, then crash-zooms back. Hard cuts every 0.3 seconds. Bodies in constant motion — hair whipping, chains swinging, hips driving, hands reaching for camera. Single-frame white flashes between cuts.  [6.0–10.0s] All three together, performing hard. The chestnut-haired woman takes the second line, singing directly to lens while the other two move behind her, bodies loose and driving. Camera orbits the trio fast, then whip-pans, then handheld snap-zooms. Cuts every 0.2 seconds. Hair, fur and fishnet all moving. Faces alive, mouths working, sweat catching the light.  [10.0–12.7s] Slow down hard, two long shots. The chestnut-haired woman alone, medium-close, singing the third line slowly and holding eye contact with the lens all the way through. Camera almost still, only breathing. Her wet hair moves across her face. She finishes the line and her mouth stays slightly open.  [12.7–15.0s] Everything detonates. Flash cutting, single-frame plates, all three thrashing and singing at once, faces close to lens, black frames and inverted negatives between cuts. Camera whipping violently. Final title hammers in: "AFTERBURN". Cut hard to black with the music.  MUSIC: driving industrial dark-pop, distorted 808 kick landing at 2.7 seconds, full arrangement through 6.0 to 10.0 seconds with clipped brass stabs and heavy tape saturation, dropping to near silence and one breath at 12.0 seconds for a full second, then the final two seconds at full weight with one metallic crash on "AFTERBURN". The lead vocal sits forward in the mix. Do not imitate an existing melody.  Warm sand-beige studio sweep, hard raking light from camera-left, Kodak Portra 400 grain, tape dropout, scanline tear, chromatic fringing, light leaks. Hard cuts only, full speed throughout, every shot carrying real motion. Titles spelled exactly as written, English only.

Only the content inside each panel may move. Preserve each panel's identity exactly: same face, same hair, same wardrobe, same jewellery, same background as in @Image 1. Panel 1 stage with mic, panel 2 graffiti alley, panel 3 studio console with bandana, panel 4 rooftop with pink car, panel 5 roller shutter with bucket hat and chains, panel 6 purple club, panel 7 sunset rooftop with braids, panel 8 neon alley, panel 9 black backdrop close-up.  [0.0-1.2s] All nine panels sit almost still like breathing photographs, only micro-movement: hair drift, blinking, smoke, light flicker. A low sub-bass swell rises. [1.2-3.0s] The beat drops. Each one takes the beat, the rest bounce in rhythm. [5.0-7.5s] Panels 4, 6, 8 and 9 answer: "Go and try it." Hands move, chains swing, ad-libs. [7.5-10.0s] All nine perform in unison, tightly lip-synced toH3, go and try it." [10.0-11.3s] The beat stops dead. [11.3-12.0s] They fire the imaginary shot on a single low hit, a tiny recoil ripples through all nine panels at once, then freeze.                Audio: one continuous hip-hop beat across all panels, roughly 92 BPM, deep 808 sub, crisp trap hats, sparse hand claps. Female voice, confident, slightly playful, not aggressive. Layer nine slightly detunedn section for a choir effect. End on one low hit plus a shorttail of room tone.  Keep the lip-sync accurate to the words in every speaking panel. beat and catch a baby/chair freeze; 12-15s complete the orbit to the front,

circle, glossy wet black floor, red backlight, acid green lasers, white strobes,

may rotate around the dancer, but the movement must stay motivated and readable:
low circular dolly, Steadicam crowd-circle wrap, low tracking inserts, and
front-angle freeze completion. Avoid random flying camera, chaotic spinning,

## 99. Dreamlike Summer Greenhouse Anime

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/minimax-h3-video-prompt-056852/video-e02bf02c9bc9.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/minimax-h3-video-prompt-056852/poster-6480d57e838e.jpg" alt="Dreamlike Summer Greenhouse Anime video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — 高品質アニメ映像。 【作品トーン】 透明感のある夏のガラス温室を舞台にした、上品で夢のような午後の叙情アニメ。映像全体は高密度な2D手描きアニメとして統一し、静かな呼吸、光の移動、素材ごとの反射と透過、繊細な表情変化を中心に描く。 【参照画像の役割】...</summary>

~~~~text
高品質アニメ映像。

【作品トーン】
透明感のある夏のガラス温室を舞台にした、上品で夢のような午後の叙情アニメ。映像全体は高密度な2D手描きアニメとして統一し、静かな呼吸、光の移動、素材ごとの反射と透過、繊細な表情変化を中心に描く。
【参照画像の役割】
画像1のみを使用する。画像1は登場する一人のキャラクターの参照として扱い、顔立ち、目の形、虹彩色、髪型、髪色、衣装、装飾、体格、全身シルエット、雰囲気、キャラクター固有色だけを参照する。

画像1そのものの背景、部屋、家具、建築、ポーズ、手の位置、画角、カメラ距離、フレーミング、構図、照明状態は再現しない。画像1の背景要素を温室内へ持ち込まない。
画像1と同じ人物を、全編で同じ一人のキャラクターとして厳密に維持する。顔の輪郭、目の形、左右の瞳の特徴、虹彩色、前髪、髪の長さ、髪型、髪飾り、衣装構造、装飾位置、体格、年齢感、キャラクター固有色を変えない。表情、視線、口元、呼吸、肩の力、手の動き、自然な髪と衣装の揺れだけ変化してよい。特徴の混合、顔の平均化、別人化、衣装交換、装飾消失、髪型変更、クローン化、分身、余計な人物の追加は禁止する。

【絵柄固定】
キャラクター同一性とは別条件として、映像全体を繊細で高密度な2D手描きアニメに固定する。細く淡い寒色系の線、透明感のある多層の色影、柔らかなグラデーション、肌の淡い発光感、髪の繊細なハイライト、瞳の多層反射を維持する。花、ガラス、紅茶、空、雲、白い家具、床タイル、温室建築も同じ密度の手描き筆致で描く。太い輪郭、簡略化されたTVアニメ調、平坦な単層セル影、低密度背景、滑らかなCG、3D化、半写実、実写化、画風混合にしない。

【キャラクターに合わせて変化させる要素】
場所と出来事は固定し、小物の意匠、アクセント色、光の印象、紅茶の色味、卓上の小さな添え物だけを画像1のキャラクターに合わせて調整する。

温室の白と淡い青を基調とした明るさは維持しながら、画像1の髪色、瞳色、衣装色、装飾色から一色だけを補助アクセントとして抽出する。アクセント色はカップの縁、ソーサーの細い装飾、卓上の花、屈折光のわずかな色味に限定し、背景全体をキャラクター色で塗り替えない。
ティーカップとソーサーの形状は、画像1の衣装や装飾の印象に合わせて、上品、可憐、端正、幻想的などの範囲でわずかに意匠を調整してよい。ただし透明なガラス製であること、カップ一つ、取っ手一つ、ソーサー一枚であることは固定する。

紅茶は透明感のある温かい色を保ち、画像1のキャラクターカラーに合わせて、琥珀色、淡い蜂蜜色、薄い薔薇色、淡い青緑を含む幻想的な茶色などへ穏やかに調整してよい。濁った液体、不透明な液体、蛍光色にはしない。
卓上の添え物は一つだけ置く。画像1の雰囲気に合わせ、柑橘の一切れ、小さな砂糖菓子、一輪の花、薄い果実片などから最も自然なものを一つ選ぶ。添え物は途中で増殖、移動、変形させない。

【固定舞台】
舞台は明るい夏のガラス温室。白い窓枠と屋根骨格、澄んだ青空、白い雲、外の緑と花、白い丸テーブル、白い金属椅子、花鉢、淡い青灰色の床タイルを維持する。白と淡い青を基調とし、日差しが作る淡い金色と、画像1から抽出した補助アクセント色だけを使用する。

【小物固定】
開始状態では、透明なガラスのティーカップ一つに紅茶が入り、誰の手にも保持されず、透明なガラスのソーサー一枚の中央に置かれている。卓上の添え物一つは白い丸テーブルの上に置いたままにする。

後半でキャラクター本人だけが右手でカップの取っ手を持つ。カップ、取っ手、紅茶、ソーサー、添え物を増殖、交換、消失、変形させない。

【開始カメラ】
最初のカメラは白い丸テーブルの斜め前、テーブル面と同じ低い高さからキャラクターの斜め前へ向く。前景にティーカップとソーサーを大きく置き、奥にキャラクターの胸上と顔を明瞭に見せる。背景消失軸は床タイルと右側の窓枠が温室奥へ伸びる方向に揃える。

紅茶とキャラクターを最初から同じ画面で紹介する。キャラクターは穏やかに呼吸し、髪先、袖、衣装の軽い装飾だけが温室の微風でわずかに揺れる。
【光の発生】
屋外の薄い雲が横へ流れて日差しが開き、温室の窓ガラスを通った白い陽光が紅茶を透過する。紅茶と透明ガラスの屈折によって生まれた柔らかな光模様が、ソーサーの縁から白いテーブルへ現れる。

光模様の基本色は淡い金色とし、画像1のキャラクター固有色がごく薄く混じる。キャラクターは新しい光に気づき、肩の力を少し抜き、上半身を光の方向へごくわずかに向ける。
【光を追うカメラ移動】
光模様が雲の移動に合わせて連続して流れ始め、カメラは光を案内線として追う。カメラ位置はテーブル斜め前から床近くへ滑らかに下がり、光の進行方向へ向いたまま、被写体サイズをカップの近景から衣装の中景へ変える。背景消失軸はテーブル奥から床タイルの斜め奥へ移す。

透明ガラスでは細い透明な反射、紅茶では色のある透過光、白い布では柔らかな拡散光、金属装飾では短い鋭い反射、床タイルでは淡い鏡面光として、素材ごとに描き分ける。

続けて光は白いテーブルから床タイル、衣装の裾や下部装飾、腰周辺の装飾へ連続して移る。カメラは床近くからキャラクターの斜め前へ向いたまま胸の高さまで上がり、被写体サイズを衣装の中景から胸上へ戻す。背景消失軸は床タイルから右上へ伸びる窓枠と屋根骨格へ受け渡す。
画像1の衣装に裾飾りや腰飾りがない場合は、存在しない装飾を追加せず、実際に存在する衣装の縁、布の重なり、胸元、袖口、髪飾りの順に光を移す。背景の窓枠、花、椅子は緩やかなパララックスを作り、温室の奥行きを保つ。

【表情変化】
光は顔中央へ入らず、肩、髪の外周、衣装の輪郭だけを淡く縁取る。

表情変化は、最初に左右の瞳の内側に元からあるキャッチライトだけが同時にわずかに明るくなり、次に眉が少し上がり、その後まぶたが柔らかく緩み、続いて口角が自然に上がり、最後に視線を紅茶へ落とす順序で見せる。
カメラは斜め前の胸上を保ち、画像1の瞳、顔立ち、髪型、髪飾り、衣装の主要装飾を読みやすく見せる。

【カップを持ち上げる動作】
キャラクターは右手の指でカップの取っ手だけを丁寧につまみ、透明なティーカップをソーサーから静かに持ち上げる。左手は白いテーブルの縁へ軽く添えたままにする。

カップを胸元より少し低い高さで保持すると、紅茶に小さな波紋が生まれ、温室の白い屋根骨格、青空、流れる雲が紅茶の表面へ自然に映り込む。
【終端カメラ】
終端のカメラはキャラクターの斜め前、胸の高さから紅茶面と顔へ向く。キャラクターを胸上の大きさで保ち、手前に紅茶面、奥に実像の三分の四顔を置く。背景消失軸は白い屋根骨格が右上へ抜ける方向に揃える。

カメラをわずかに下向きへ傾け、キャラクターが透明なティーカップを胸元より少し低い位置で静かに保持する。手前の紅茶面に温室の青空と、同じキャラクターの三分の四顔が一度だけ明瞭に映る。

紅茶面の顔は光学的な反射像であり、別人、分身、第二の人物ではない。奥の実像と同じ顔立ち、髪型、表情、視線方向を保つ。奥の実像は穏やかな微笑みを維持し、紅茶の波紋が収まり、実像と反射像が同時に澄んだ瞬間に切る。

【禁止事項】
光点、水平フレア、ガラス反射を顔中央、鼻筋、眉間、瞳の表面へ重ねない。顔を光で隠さない。背景を画像1の背景へ置き換えない。温室以外の場所へ変更しない。文字、字幕、ロゴ、透かしなし。BGMなし、音楽なし、環境音なし、フォーリーなし、音響生成なし。
~~~~

</details>

## 100. Fantasy Academy Bread-Sharing Scene

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/minimax-h3-video-prompt-773075/video-85ff5049786e.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/minimax-h3/minimax-h3-video-prompt-773075/poster-702c97b9b772.jpg" alt="Fantasy Academy Bread-Sharing Scene video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — 高品質アニメ映像。世界観は、上品な現代ファンタジー学園を舞台にした2D手描きの日常会話アニメ。表情設計は必ず、目 → 眉 → まぶた → 口 → 視線、の順で行う。 【参照画像と人物固定】...</summary>

~~~~text
高品質アニメ映像。世界観は、上品な現代ファンタジー学園を舞台にした2D手描きの日常会話アニメ。表情設計は必ず、目 → 眉 → まぶた → 口 → 視線、の順で行う。

【参照画像と人物固定】
画像1は人物Aの参照画像。画像1に描かれたキャラクターを、全編で同じ人物として維持する。画像1は、人物Aの顔、目の形、虹彩色、髪型、髪色、衣装、装飾、体格、シルエット、雰囲気、キャラクターカラーの参照にのみ使用する。
画像2は人物Bの参照画像。画像2に描かれたキャラクターを、全編で同じ人物として維持する。画像2は、人物Bの顔、目の形、虹彩色、髪型、髪色、衣装、装飾、体格、シルエット、雰囲気、キャラクターカラーの参照にのみ使用する。

参照画像そのものの背景、部屋、家具、ポーズ、画角、フレーミング、光源配置は再現しない。変化してよいのは、表情、視線、口元、姿勢、呼吸、自然な髪と衣装の揺れだけ。

人物Aと人物Bの特徴を混ぜない。顔の平均化、衣装交換、髪型交換、別人化、クローン化、分身、余計な人物の追加は禁止。

画像2に武器や手持ち品が含まれていても、人物Bの両手には何も持たせない。画像1に非戦闘用の装飾や小型バッグがある場合のみ、人物Aに自然に残してよい。ただしバッグは身体側に固定し、手で持つ、開ける、動作に使うことは禁止。武器、大型装備、発光する戦闘装備は持たせない。

【小物・色味・雰囲気の調整】
場所、座る位置、会話内容、パンを分ける動作、カメラ構成は固定する。そのうえで、人物Aと人物Bの参照画像に合わせて、髪飾り、ブローチ、リボン、バッグなどの装飾、小物、配色、光の印象、背景の差し色を自然に調整する。

全体の線色、影色、背景の差し色、午後光の色温度は、人物Aと人物Bのキャラクターカラーと雰囲気に合わせて調整する。ただし、明るく上品で清潔な現代ファンタジー学園の日常感は維持し、暗い戦闘空間、ホラー、退廃、極端な逆光にはしない。
【絵柄固定】
細く繊細な色付き線画、透明感のある二層から三層の色影、柔らかなグラデーション。彩度は低くしすぎず、参照人物の固有色を髪、瞳、衣装、反射光、背景の差し色へ自然に反映する。

瞳は宝石のような虹彩の奥行きと複数の小さなキャッチライト、髪は真珠のような多層ハイライト。髪、まつ毛、レース、刺繍、髪飾り、装飾、衣服の皺を高密度に描く。布は上質なマット素材、レース、金属、宝石、リボンはそれぞれ異なる繊細な質感で描き分ける。

背景も同じ2D手描き筆致で、クリーム色の壁、木製ベンチ、植栽、遠景の校舎を十分な密度で描く。太線、厚輪郭、簡略TVアニメ化、平坦な単層セル塗り、低密度背景、滑らかな3D素材、半写実、実写質感、強いくすみ、生成AI平均顔、画風混合は禁止。
【固定場所と人物配置】
放課後の学園中庭にある木製ベンチ。人物Bは画面左、人物Aは画面右。二人は隣り合って座る。

人物Aだけが、薄い紙包みに入った丸いあんパン一個を右手で下側から持つ。開始時のパンは完全な丸形で、歯形も欠けもない。指は紙包みの下側に置き、食べる位置から離す。パン、紙包み、手指を増やさない。

【Cut 1】
カメラ位置はベンチ正面中央。カメラ高は座った二人の目線の高さ。正面から二人を胸上で同じ大きさに収め、背景の消失軸は二人の間の奥へ抜ける。目的は、二人の関係とパンの持ち主が人物Aであることを示すこと。

人物Bはパンを見たあと人物Aへ視線を戻し、両手を胸元で合わせ、身体ごと少し近づく。瞳を輝かせ、眉を上げ、まぶたを期待で開き、甘える口元を作り、人物Aへ視線を合わせる。
人物Bだけが明るく甘い声で言う。
「そのアンパン、一口ちょうだい。」

短い止めを入れ、さらに甘えて言う。
「お願い、一生のお願い！」

人物Aは話さず、あきれながらも優しい目で人物Bを見る。
【Cut 2】
カメラ位置は人物Aの斜め前。カメラ高は二人の目線の高さ。人物Aの肩越しから人物Bへ向き、二人の顔とパンを中近景で見せる。背景の消失軸は右奥へ変える。目的は、返答、差し出す手、一口だけかじる動作を一続きで見せること。

人物Aは瞳を柔らかくし、眉を少し下げ、まぶたを穏やかに細め、困った笑顔の口元を作り、人物Bへ視線を合わせる。

小さく息を吐き、短い止めを入れ、人物Aだけが落ち着いた優しい声で言う。
「もう、しょうがないなー。ひとくちだけだよ」
言い終わりながら、人物Aは右手の一個のあんパンを人物Bの口元へ差し出す。持ち主は人物Aのままで、手渡さない。

接触前：パンは完全な丸形。人物Bの歯とパンの間に小さな隙間がある。
接触：人物Bが顔を近づけ、歯がパンの上側の端へ接触する瞬間を見せ、一口だけかじる。

接触後：パンにだけ小さな半月形の一口分が欠け、人物Bは顔を離す。人物Aは同じパンを右手で胸元へ戻す。手や指を噛まない。パン、紙包み、手指を複製しない。

【Cut 3】
カメラ位置は人物Bの斜め前。カメラ高は二人の目線の高さ。人物B側から人物A側へ向き、人物Bを胸上でやや大きく見せたあと、短く左へ移動して二人の胸上ツーショットにする。背景の消失軸は左奥へ変える。目的は、人物Bの満足顔と人物Aが返す微笑みを同じ終端画へ置くこと。
人物Bは口を閉じて小さく二回だけ噛み、飲み込む。短い止めのあと、瞳を明るくし、眉を柔らかな弧にし、まぶたを幸せそうに細め、満足した笑顔の口元を作り、人物Aへ視線を戻す。

人物Bだけが弾む優しい声で言う。
「んー、おいしい！」

人物Aは一口分だけ欠けた同じパンを右手で胸元に持ち、無言で温かな微笑みを返す。二人の顔とパンを同じ画面に保ち、人物Bが「おいしい」の最後の母音を言い終え、二人の笑顔が完成した瞬間に切る。
【音声】
人物の外見、年齢感、雰囲気に合った日本語の二人の声を生成する。人物Bは明るく甘い声、人物Aは落ち着いた優しい声。話者を交換せず、三つの台詞を一字一句そのまま、重ねず、口の形と発声を同期させる。

台詞以外の会話、ナレーション、字幕、画面内文字は入れない。BGMなし、音楽なし。環境音とフォーリーのみ。弱い風、遠い鳥、布擦れ、紙包みの紙音、柔らかなパン生地を一口かじった瞬間の小さな圧縮音を、見えている動作へ同期させる。誇張した咀嚼音は入れない。

【重要な禁止】
話者交換、同時発話、口パクずれ、パンの複製、勝手な手渡し、二口以上食べる、食べる前の歯形、手指の変形、人物の追加、顔を隠す前景、顔中央の光点、字幕、ロゴを出さない。
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
The man sings the song with great emotion and energy. His movements are rapid and in sync with the music. He looks straight ahead. He is full of drive and energy, and his arm movements complement the motion of his body. Handheld shooting.
He sings passionately and emotionally with perfect lip sync the lyrics: “My chest goes quick My pulse on fire One little smile And I climb higher I don’t stand still I don’t stay calm You turn my whole Body on.”
